# Validation log — October 2026

Monthly validation log split from [docs/validation.md](../validation.md#current-validation-guidance).

## October 29 night the commit action waits for the busy look, menus open clear of their button and row, and an ended pause says so

`56fa8ea`, `ddf33d5` and `5b6a794` let the remaining write controls, the commit action and the Tags, Stashes and rebase dialogs look busy only once a write has run a moment, `2b4a120` makes Quick Open's failure an alert on Enter and `13ff430` keeps a button's menu clear of its button; `94fcce1` draws a travelling row beneath the section headings and `331d474` brings the worktree manager's Create form stops into view; `ec43e36` takes the pressed control's tooltip away with its row, `4fbfe6d` reveals Settings' first control every time the page opens and `24f5962` keeps a one-match shortcut search whole and counts it as one; `c470e3b`, `ddb8454`, `1c48137`, `def8aed`, `478cc8d`, `4aa1e23`, `833caa8` and `77950a7` write an open failure's short line from its category, say why an image side has no image whatever the room, give both captions' open controls one place and let Compare's footer give way before an image's minimum, and `f3cbe7d`, `cffac46` and `81db2e3` keep the image band's alert in one parent node, the side's reason out of the image's node and the captions' words on a drawn node; `04acd36`, `d81342e`, `8f63158` and `fa1be3b` keep a right-click menu off its row and in step with writes, return its focus when its row goes, open no keyboard menu from inside it and let it go when closed; `b9dc438`, `2f7f5e0` and `021f61b` refuse a commit's recovery actions while an integration is paused, read a cached pause again when a recovery action refuses for it, and refuse Tags' actions wherever the dialog announces them refused; and `c85f521` announces a refused local workspace name at once.

- **Automated:** each commit's tests; fast gate green after the night's integration, the product code of `9483bbd` being `fa1be3b`. The release measurements of the Changes body and of image comparison redraws are recorded separately ([Changes body](../benchmarks/2026-10-29-changes-body-redraw.md), [image comparison](../benchmarks/2026-10-29-image-compare-redraw.md)).
- **Host and capture:** Asahi Omarchy (Hyprland 0.56.2, native Wayland, scale 2; frames are physical pixels, figures below are logical), windows forced opaque, Midnight unless noted (the Omarchy tokyo-night theme seeded in a per-launch home), notifications silenced, empty XDG homes per launch, accessibility bus on. Fixtures under `/tmp/gitturtle-evidence/`: the principles fixtures (`pr/work` for row writes, `pr/dirty` for Working Actions), the compact fixture, `q8/slow` (an unreachable origin for a slow Fetch), `q8/changes`, `q8/dlg`, `q9/slowdlg`, `q9/merge/rest` and `q9/merge/paused` (merges started and aborted outside the app), `q9/wtb` (a main worktree and free branches b1 to b8), `q9/rows`, `q9/model` and `q9/model2`, `sysprev` and `sysprev-mod`, and the offline GitHub review fixture. Windows from 461 × 280 to 1,480 × 980, tiled and floating, at 13 and 18 pt. Driver `tools/run-q9.sh` with `make-q9.sh` and `steps/q9-*.sh`; bursts are back-to-back region grabs.
- **Builds:** base `gitturtle-62d10ae` (sha256 `2377cfcd…`), candidate `gitturtle-9483bbd` (sha256 `a93dd785…`), both clean aarch64 releases; each executable's `--build-info` and embedded commit (`strings`) checked, and both digests rechecked against their recorded hashes before the run.
- **Checks:** 29, in seven groups: A, writes wait for the busy look (1–5); B, row travel and the worktree Create form (6–7); C, tooltips, Settings and Shortcuts (8–10); D, System preview (11–15); E, right-click menus (16–18); F, the previous review's unreached items (19–25); G, the night's fix rounds (26–29). After the design review's corrections: 21 pass, 1 fail (the same in the base), 6 partial and 1 not reached.
- **Results:** **A** (3 pass, 1 partial, 1 not reached). A quick Unstage keeps the commit button at "Write a commit title" in all 120 frames of two presses, where the base drew "Working..." for exactly one frame of each (+124 and +142 ms) ([commit button](../evidence/busy-look-menus-and-paused-refusals/commit-button-13pt-no-working-frame.png)); in a slow Fetch the candidate shows "Working...", the grey picker and profile, dimmed Fetch, Pull and Push, the progress line and Cancel operation together in one frame, where the base greyed the picker and profile at +176 ms and dimmed Fetch, Pull and Push only at +432. A quick Stage or Unstage leaves the branch picker and profile without busy grey in all 120 header frames and 60 full frames, but the base's one-frame flash was not caught in this run's header frames either, so check 1 is partial: the fix is not shown against the base. Quick Open's failure is a polite status while typing, once per pause, and one assertive alert on Enter (bus only; the base had no node). At 18 pt in 461 × 330 the Working Actions menu opens 4.5 px above its button, where the base laid it over the button, and a re-tile closes it ([dropdown](../evidence/busy-look-menus-and-paused-refusals/dropdown-18pt-461x330-above-its-button.png)); in taller tiles both builds open it below. **B** (1 pass, 1 partial). At 1,480 × 980 13 pt the unstaged row travels beneath the Unstaged heading, which stays whole, where the base drew the row over it ([row travel](../evidence/busy-look-menus-and-paused-refusals/travelling-row-13pt-beneath-unstaged-heading.png)); the 461 × 490 18 pt case could not be started. The worktree Create form rings all 26 stops whole at 13 and 18 pt, Tab reaches b8 with b7 above it and a scroll cue, a reopened choice list starts at b1 again, and "No worktrees match this filter." is whole at 19.5 and 27 px tall where the base drew 3 px ([no match](../evidence/busy-look-menus-and-paused-refusals/worktree-no-match-13pt-line-whole.png)). **C** (3 pass). Under a still pointer no stale "Stage gamma.txt" tooltip appears at 0.6, 1.0 or 1.5 s (the base showed it at all three), and Commit's and Fetch's tooltips still come; Ctrl+, after Escape at 18 pt in 461 × 490 with Omarchy tokyo-night focuses the Omarchy card in view, where the base's was below the fold ([Settings](../evidence/busy-look-menus-and-paused-refusals/settings-ctrl-comma-18pt-omarchy-card-in-view.png)); a one-match Shortcuts search reads "1 shortcut" with the entry and its Ctrl+Shift+P chip whole, where the base cut the entry by 18 px, and the General heading's top padding is now cut instead (N8; [one match](../evidence/busy-look-menus-and-paused-refusals/shortcuts-18pt-one-match-heading-cut.png)). **D** (3 pass, 2 partial). An added image's Before side reads "No image on this side" side by side at 461 and 1,480 px, with the image 131 and 142 px (base nothing, 107 and 109 px) ([absent side](../evidence/busy-look-menus-and-paused-refusals/image-13pt-absent-side-says-no-image.png)), and once on the bus; Overlay at 1,480 px places it beneath "Before"; Overlay at 461 px was not reached. A modified image at 461 × 490 13 pt gets 125 px (base 101); a 1,061 → 501 px resize never draws the old labelled toolbar (the base drew it for a frame); at 18 pt both open controls now sit beneath the names and the image gets 94 px of its 133 px minimum, from 61 (partial, N6). **E** (3 pass). A second right-click then Escape returns focus to the selected row; at 461 × 490 History's lowest row's menu ends 1 px above the row at 13 and 18 pt, where the base covered it by 27 and 47 px ([right-click](../evidence/busy-look-menus-and-paused-refusals/history-row-menu-18pt-above-its-row.png)); Shift-F10's Revert during a paused merge is refused with its message, the same as the base, so check 18 passes only as no regression. **F** (5 pass, 1 fail, 1 partial). The Stashes Tab walk with a stash selected rings every stop and scrolls the detail; the 180 px navigation's 2 px bar is 7.48:1 in Midnight and 4.41:1 in Daylight; a dropdown's second click closes it with nothing chosen; the inspector pinned and unpinned while the tile narrows from 1,000 to 461 px is pixel-identical between the builds; the refusal band is 42 px between its rules at 640 × 430 and 461 × 490 with its full tooltip whole, and at 640 × 430 the image keeps 64.5 px under it as the footer gives way. GitHub #42 opened at 13 pt only (partial; identical in both builds), and the Reflog details still take 41 Tabs from a selected entry in both builds (fail, N4). **G** (3 pass, 1 partial). The image comparison's tree keeps the image-less side's line beside the image node and the band's alert under one panel at every height from 490 to 220 px; a right-click menu whose last row goes returns focus to the list, and Shift-F10 or the Menu key inside it opens no second menu (the base drew two); the stash form replaces a stale paused-integration refusal with the muted "The paused operation has ended. Review again to continue.", in the refusal's place on one line, announced once politely ([ended line](../evidence/busy-look-menus-and-paused-refusals/stash-form-13pt-paused-operation-ended-line.png)). Document headers' reason line (check 26) was not reached: no document open fails on this host.
- **Review:** a read-only design review verified the claims above against the frames, corrected two QA calls (check 1 from pass to partial; check 18 a pass only as no regression) and two fix directions (N3, N6), and checked the crops for privacy. **Regression N1:** with no file watcher, after a merge paused and then aborted outside the app, Stashes' Review restore… is refused from the cached pause: the refusal is announced but its line is gone within 0.3 s as the quiet read lands, and the Stashes dialog closes with its selection lost, where the base opened the Restore stash review, rightly since the merge had ended ([N1](../evidence/busy-look-menus-and-paused-refusals/stashes-review-restore-13pt-stale-pause-refusal.png), 50 % scale). **Defects, queued** in [item 9](../development/omarchy-full-support.md) (several fixes in progress): Changes cannot be opened while a write runs, yet its toggle looks and is announced available (N2); the Stashes list is never a Tab stop and its second stash is wholly clipped with no scroll cue at 461 × 490 (N3); the Reflog's 41 Tabs (N4); after the last row goes under its menu the cursor restarts at the top (N5); the 18 pt image controls (N6); narrow model headers stack past the window's foot and leave the model 2 px (N7); the Shortcuts heading row's height (N8); a right-click menu on a deleted non-last working row stays open over the next row (N9); the paused-merge refusal says "resolve conflicts" with none unresolved (N10); and the design review's own findings on the worktree no-match line's colour and period, the absent image side's wording, the menus' two gaps and the ended line's wording.
- **Limitations:** implementers were compiling throughout (load 9–28), so this entry claims no latency; frame intervals ran 30–120 ms for full windows and 8–24 ms for strips, so a state shorter than one interval would be missed, and the slow write's first busy frame (about 250–270 ms after the press) cannot be judged against the 200 ms delay and needs an idle re-measure. Not reached: check 5 (no native route opens a dialog while a write starts: during a write the palette refuses Tags and the rebase review and Changes, the only route to Stashes, does not open); check 6 at 461 × 490 18 pt (no single-row flight can start there with the heading in view); Overlay at 461 px (check 11); GitHub #42 at 18 pt, its wrap offset and Reply's scroll (check 19); and check 26, since every document open succeeds or offers no control on this host. Quick Open's announcements and the ended line were read on the bus only; no screen reader was run. `scripts/native_qa/qa.py` was unavailable on this host: it needs python-xlib and XWayland, which this Hyprland host does not provide; the frames come from the session's Wayland drivers instead.
- **Evidence:** [`docs/evidence/busy-look-menus-and-paused-refusals/`](../evidence/busy-look-menus-and-paused-refusals).

## October 29 evening write controls wait to look busy, the status line keeps whole outcomes, and dialogs keep one left edge

`eee5d3f` (with `8804af9`, `eefa8ba`, `8736a3c`, `3e21893`, `e7b8085` and `2d41682`) lets a write look busy only once it has run a moment, announces the refusing controls unavailable before they dim and lights nothing the Changes rows move under a still pointer; `be8b53b`, `45126c7` and `fd519a7` keep a fresh outcome and the ahead and behind counts whole and the Stashes restore actions in view; `b0a5b25`, `9aa5320` and `c28a251` keep the refresh warning whole with everything else fitted around it; `03f7b1a`, `7302a71`, `ea58f87`, `9762e6d`, `8509974` and `84afda7` light no row a wheel moves, keep every menu's edge 8 px above the status bar and let Enter act only on an item that can act; `76b0c28`, `74ee111` and `1900878` give dialogs their own edge over a deeper dimmed page; `1a41daf`, `1ee73fc`, `891f109` and `95fabee` start Tags' and Worktrees' rows at their text inset and bring the Reflog's Tab stops into view; `35b1cb5`, `13e3088`, `7e63c52`, `d70e77f` and `f9f6aa3` read dialog hints and Projects by their lines and report what static text draws; `df785d7`, `1795a14` and `0d93e96` open Settings and the worktree manager with focus drawn and in view; `5755f3d`, `7d30d48`, `d197b3c` and `2957703` give an image's dimensions way before the image and say why an open failed in a short line on its own side; `b5f43fa` and `99fc9e0` put the page back on the accessibility bus when its dialog closes; `be50c01` and `8eb2fe2` reveal the GitHub panel's focus whole; `12b7043` lays the Changes body out for its own height; and `61e8df7`, `75203c6`, `5647d11` and `9609d2a` keep History's changed files drawn while their filter answers and write the inspector's width only from a drag.

- **Automated:** each commit's tests; fast gate green at `9609d2a`, the product code of `62d10ae`.
- **Host and capture:** Asahi Omarchy (Hyprland, native Wayland, scale 2; frames are physical pixels, figures below are logical), windows forced opaque, Midnight unless noted (Braden and the Omarchy catppuccin-latte and tokyo-night themes seeded in per-launch homes), notifications silenced, empty XDG homes per launch, accessibility bus on. Fixtures under `/tmp/gitturtle-evidence/`: the principles fixtures (`pr/work` for row writes, `pr/dirty` for Working Actions), the compact fixture, `q7/up` (Fetch with nothing new and a tracking branch), `q7/stash1`, `q8/slow` (an unreachable origin for a slow Fetch), `q8/changes`, `q8/many` (594 changed files), `q8/dlg` (42 HEAD reflog entries, two stashes, one tag), `q8/wt2` and `q8/wt4`, `sysprev` and `sysprev-mod`, and the offline GitHub review fixture; the watch warning with inotify instances set to 0 for the app's user namespace only. Tiled at 461 × 490 to 1,000 × 490, 640 × 430 and 640 × 680, floating at 1,480 × 800, 1,480 × 980 and 1,000 × 680, at 13 and 18 pt. Driver `tools/run-q8.sh`; row-write bursts of 24 frames about 20 ms apart.
- **Builds:** base `gitturtle-7abe8b2` (sha256 `259c7b82…`), candidate `gitturtle-62d10ae` (sha256 `2377cfcd…`), both clean releases; each executable's `--build-info` and embedded commit (`strings`) checked.
- **Results:** in Working Actions at 461 × 490, one wheel notch under a still pointer lights no row where the base lit two, and a 1 px move lights exactly one ([one notch](../evidence/write-busy-look-status-line-and-dialog-edges/wheel-notch-13pt-no-row-lit.png)). A right-click on History's lowest whole row opens its menu 8.5 px above the status bar at 13 and 18 pt, where the base ran 19.5 and 29.5 px over it ([18 pt](../evidence/write-busy-look-status-line-and-dialog-edges/history-row-menu-18pt-8.5px-above-bar.png)); a raised keyboard menu, History's and the Changes row menu at 18 pt, ends exactly at its row's top (the Changes menu covered its row before); the commit menu opens at the row's trailing end, its visible edge 13.5 px inside the list at 18 pt, with lanes to its left and no next row's ellipsis to its right; Enter on a disabled item leaves the menu open with nothing run, where the base closed it. In Braden the dialog's edge is #b8c1cb, 1.68:1 on the dialog and 1.41:1 on the dimmed page (was #d2dce8, 1.28:1), catppuccin-latte's #adb0ba is 1.92:1, and Midnight keeps #2b3749 over a deeper page (#080a0f canvas, #0b0f15 panel; was #0d1119 and #121822) ([Braden](../evidence/write-busy-look-status-line-and-dialog-edges/braden-dialog-edge-and-dimmed-page.png)); Keyboard Shortcuts' scrollbar track sits on the raised surface. A Stage or Unstage from a row keeps the header, the tab and Stage all in their resting colours, with no "Staging file…" or Cancel, and the list's foot and the composer's top stay put in all 24 frames of both bursts in both builds. A slow Fetch reports the write controls unavailable on the bus about 40 ms after the press and draws them dimmed 221–231 ms after that (base 9–26 ms), on History's Latest, Targets' fields and Activity too; an Actions menu opened in that window lists its five items disabled, and Browse reflog… pressed then leaves Activity open. At 18 pt in 461 × 490, Fetch with nothing new keeps "Up to date with origin · nothing new" whole (216.5 px) beside the keys-only hints "Ctrl+F · ↑↓ · Enter", where the base cut it to "Up to date with ori…" ([outcome](../evidence/write-busy-look-status-line-and-dialog-edges/fetch-nothing-new-18pt-461-outcome-whole.png)); the counts read "↑ 0  ↓ 0" in digits beside "main" and "✓ feature" (base "↑ …  ↓ …") and are named "0 ahead, 0 behind" on the bus. In Stashes at 1,000 × 680 18 pt, Restore staged state, Drop… and Review restore… stand whole above the dialog's note on selection and stay put while the detail scrolls (the base put Restore staged state past Close). With the watch warning at 18 pt, "Some changes need Refresh" and Refresh stay whole at every width while the hints give way in order, whole at 1,000 and 820 px, keys only at 700 and 600, fewer at 520 and none at 461 (the base clipped them mid-word at the window's edge from 600 px down); a fresh outcome stays whole down to 600 px and ends in an ellipsis only at 520 and 461 px, once every hint is gone, with Details… as its icon beside it and its label at rest, as DESIGN.md sets ([600 px](../evidence/write-busy-look-status-line-and-dialog-edges/watch-warning-18pt-600-outcome-whole.png)); Tab to Refresh and Details… draws whole rings inside the bar. Escape from the tracking-branch review leaves no "Unknown object" of the frame's 31 children at 1,480 × 800 13 pt and 29 at 461 × 490 18 pt, also 3 s later, after switching pages and after Confirm (base 23 of 30 and 21 of 28; bus only). Tags names "1 local tag" and starts its title, summary, field and row on one left edge, where the base centred the row, with "Filter tags…" as the 18 pt placeholder and "No local tags yet · Create tag… names a commit" with none ([Tags](../evidence/write-busy-look-status-line-and-dialog-edges/tags-13pt-one-left-edge.png)); Worktrees holds both rows whole on the same edge, where the base cut the second; in the Reflog each of twenty Tab stops comes into view with a whole ring, where the base never scrolled and left the tenth off-screen, and the list's and the dialog's thumbs both show, the list's 7.5 px clear of the ring ([tenth stop](../evidence/write-busy-look-status-line-and-dialog-edges/reflog-13pt-tenth-tab-stop-in-view.png)). On the bus the palette, Quick Open ("18 matches · ↑/↓ select · Enter opens · Escape cancels"), Keyboard Shortcuts ("61 shortcuts · Ctrl, Alt and Shift" and its hint) and Stashes' lines are read by their text, where the base had none of these labels; Projects' first frame rings Open whole, and the inspector's 18 pt title ends at the document's edge, not 28 px past it. After one wheel notch at 18 pt, Shortcuts draws a thumb, and after a one-match search the thumb stays, correctly, since the list still overflows. Settings opened by its button or Ctrl+, rings Follow system appearance in view at 13 and 18 pt (the base focused nothing), and with an Omarchy theme the button rings the selected card, which a pointer press clears ([18 pt](../evidence/write-busy-look-status-line-and-dialog-edges/settings-shortcut-18pt-switch-ring.png)); Escape and Back return focus to History; the worktree manager's Tab and Shift+Tab bring every stop into view with its ring at 461 × 490. In System preview at 461 × 490 13 pt the captions drop "96 × 64 · PNG" to a tooltip and the bus, and the image grows from 83 to 101 px ([captions](../evidence/write-busy-look-status-line-and-dialog-edges/system-preview-13pt-captions-image-taller.png)); a refused open reads "Seen as an / SVG docum…" in After's half with the image kept (64 px), where the base filled the side with the sentence and drew no image ([refusal](../evidence/write-busy-look-status-line-and-dialog-edges/refusal-13pt-band-absent-side-note-gone.png)); at 640 × 430 the image under the band is drawn whole, as intended; a cancelled chooser reads "Nothing opened." on one line in both builds; and the 18 pt title "Add polyglot.png" takes two lines, was three. Reopening the GitHub panel at 18 pt in 461 × 490 scrolls the focused destination field to the pane's top with a whole ring (the base left it at y 222.5). Typing fast into the changed-files filter of a 594-file commit never shows an empty list, "No matches", "Filtering…" or "Path filter unavailable", and a 480 px inspector draws 155.5 px at 461 × 490 18 pt and is 480 again when relaunched wide; inspector and filter look the same as in the base. The Changes body shows no visible change: no jump of the list or composer at 13 or 18 pt, and the description hands focus to the title at the same heights as in the base.
- **Review:** a read-only design review verified the claims above against the frames, corrected several QA calls (the commit menu's visible edge, not its border; the 640 × 430 image; Details… at rest) and checked the crops for privacy. **Defects, queued** in [item 9](../development/omarchy-full-support.md) (fixes in progress): a quick Stage or Unstage still draws the branch picker and the profile in the busy grey and the commit button as "Working…" for one frame (about 25 ms) while the write runs ([one frame](../evidence/write-busy-look-status-line-and-dialog-edges/unstage-one-frame-busy-picker-profile-working.png), candidate frames at +111, +134 and +176 ms); under a still pointer the pressed control's tooltip "Stage gamma.txt" stands over the row that moved beneath it from 1.02 s until the pointer moves ([stale tooltip](../evidence/write-busy-look-status-line-and-dialog-edges/still-pointer-stale-stage-gamma-tooltip.png)); Ctrl+, with an Omarchy theme at 18 pt in 461 × 490 focuses the card below the fold; at 461 px the absent side's "No image on this side" is gone from Before's caption, a regression from `5755f3d` (visible in the [refusal](../evidence/write-busy-look-status-line-and-dialog-edges/refusal-13pt-band-absent-side-note-gone.png) crop); at 18 pt only Before's open control wraps, leaving the image 61 px (base 12); the one Shortcuts match is cut at the list's foot and the status reads "1 shortcuts" (both builds); the inspector's two misses at a 1,404 px window (454.5 px with Actions as text) and Compare's long paths ("…n/RepositoryNav.tsx", File actions beside the path) are the same in the base; reopening the GitHub panel scrolls the offline notice out of view (taste); and, in both builds, Settings' "Follow system appearance" heading wraps beside its switch's own label at 18 pt in 461 px.
- **Limitations:** implementers were compiling throughout (load 5–17), so this entry claims no motion or latency timing; the busy look's 221–231 ms after the bus is a burst reading, not a measurement. The Changes body's release measurement is owed separately. Not reached: GitHub #42 (the fixture's pull requests were not refreshed first), the Reflog's entry-selected Tab walk, the Stashes Tab walk with a stash selected, the 2 px navigation accent bar at 180 px, a dropdown's second click on its trigger, the inspector pinned and unpinned while its width changes, and the refusal band's heights and tooltip; the Orca sub-check was skipped and no screen reader was run. `scripts/native_qa/qa.py` was unavailable on this host: it needs python-xlib and XWayland, which this Hyprland host does not provide; the frames come from the session's Wayland drivers instead.
- **Evidence:** [`docs/evidence/write-busy-look-status-line-and-dialog-edges/`](../evidence/write-busy-look-status-line-and-dialog-edges).

## October 29 keyboard menus clear the status bar, capped menus keep whole rows, and Fetch says when nothing is new

`03518e9` keeps keyboard row menus above the status bar, `aebe65f` keeps the Stashes dialog's lines whole in a narrow window, `9c24a9a` keeps a capped menu's rows whole and shows its scrollbar, `504421a` confirms Stage all in its resting colour, `92b48e2` says a Changes filter matched nothing inside its list, `2ea8f29` ends History's new-commit cue once a followed read shows it, `19ba13f` (on `d0c66fe`, `7665f1c`, `520f384`) says when Fetch or Pull found nothing new, `16aff37` confirms a created tracking branch on the branch picker, and `1d5c1f4` releases a context menu with its element; `d75fd70`'s one-resize layout with no selection, the navigator's row menu and the author's tooltip, not reached in the previous entry, are checked here.

- **Automated:** each commit's tests; fast gate green at `7abe8b2`.
- **Host and capture:** Asahi Omarchy (Hyprland, native Wayland, scale 2; frames are physical pixels, figures below are logical), windows forced opaque, Midnight, notifications silenced, empty XDG homes per launch, accessibility bus on (off for idle CPU). Fixtures under `/tmp/gitturtle-evidence/`: the compact fixture (History's commit menu), the principles fixtures (Changes menus, Stage all, the filter), `q7/stash1` and `q7/nostash` (Stashes), `q7/cue` (a commit made outside the app, then Push), `q7/up` (a local bare origin with `main` upstream, `feature` and `release` fetched once, and a second clone that pushes) and the history-scroll fixture (one resize from 1,480 to 1,000 px after a search with no match). Tiled at 461 × 490 and floating at 1,480 × 800, 1,480 × 980 and 1,000 × 680, at 13 and 18 pt. Driver `run-q7.sh`; idle CPU `idle-cpu.sh`.
- **Builds:** base `a79b83c` (sha256 `87d8c9e1…`), candidate `7abe8b2` (sha256 `259c7b82…`; product code `e40894c`), both clean releases; the one-resize step compares base `9ed2f45` (sha256 `544a3606…`) with `a79b83c`.
- **Results:** at 18 pt in 461 × 490, Shift+F10 and the Menu key on History's row 39 open its menu 7.0 px above the status bar, where it ended 2.0 px above ([history row menu](../evidence/capped-menus-stashes-and-up-to-date/history-row-menu-18pt-2px-to-7px-above-bar.png)); the Changes row and commit menus, which ran over the bar, now end above it ([Changes row menu](../evidence/capped-menus-stashes-and-up-to-date/changes-row-menu-18pt-off-the-bar-onto-its-row.png), [commit menu](../evidence/capped-menus-stashes-and-up-to-date/commit-menu-18pt-off-the-bar.png)); a press on the bar closes the menu and leaves focus on the row in both builds. With one stash, Stashes reads "Choose a stash to inspect its files." on one whole line on the dialog's text edge (two centred lines before, the second cut), and with none it drops the empty detail so the list takes the room (163.5 px at 18 pt, was 89.5; 219.5 px at 13 pt, was 64) ([one stash](../evidence/capped-menus-stashes-and-up-to-date/one-stash-18pt-prompt-whole.png), [no stash](../evidence/capped-menus-stashes-and-up-to-date/no-stash-18pt-list-takes-the-room.png)); a selected stash's detail scrolls alone. The Working Actions menu's pitch is 27.5 px at 13 pt (24.5 before, rows squeezed) and it ends on a whole row with a visible scrollbar; at 18 pt it ends after a whole row and scrolls to "Undo last local commit…", where the base cut its last row and barely scrolled ([13 pt](../evidence/capped-menus-stashes-and-up-to-date/actions-menu-13pt-whole-rows-and-scrollbar.png), [18 pt](../evidence/capped-menus-stashes-and-up-to-date/actions-menu-18pt-whole-rows-and-scrollbar.png)). The "Staged" confirmation on an emptied Unstaged heading is #e8eef7 on #131a25 (14.98:1), not the disabled #606a7a (3.19:1), and returns to the disabled colour afterwards in both builds ([13 pt](../evidence/capped-menus-stashes-and-up-to-date/stage-all-13pt-staged-3.19-to-14.98.png), [after](../evidence/capped-menus-stashes-and-up-to-date/stage-all-13pt-after-disabled-in-both.png), [18 pt icon](../evidence/capped-menus-stashes-and-up-to-date/unstage-all-icon-18pt.png)). A filter with no match says "No files match this filter" inside the list with Clear filter beneath and nothing above the list moving (the base put the line above it and pushed the list down 21 px); at 18 pt it reads "No matches" with a 39 px icon Clear filter, where the base showed nothing; both are named in full on the bus ([13 pt](../evidence/capped-menus-stashes-and-up-to-date/filter-none-13pt-line-inside-list.png), [18 pt](../evidence/capped-menus-stashes-and-up-to-date/filter-none-18pt-no-matches-and-clear.png)). After Push from History, Latest drops "· 1 new commit" and the status line clears (the base kept both over the new top row); the cue's dot is 7.0 px across, was 8.5, and its ink sits 10.3 px from the arrow's, was 6.4 ([dot](../evidence/capped-menus-stashes-and-up-to-date/cued-dot-clears-the-arrow.png), [after Push](../evidence/capped-menus-stashes-and-up-to-date/after-push-cue-ends.png)). Fetch with nothing new says "Up to date with origin · nothing new" and Pull "Up to date with origin/main · nothing new" (the base said "Fetched origin" and "Pulled origin/main"); after a push from the second clone both builds say "Fetched origin" and "Pulled origin/main" ([13 pt](../evidence/capped-menus-stashes-and-up-to-date/fetch-nothing-new-13pt-status.png)). A tracking branch created and switched to shows "✓ feature" on the picker with the tooltip "Created · feature" and the status "Created and switched to feature · tracks origin/feature", without the band that pushed the header down 41 px at 1,480 and 67 px at 461; created without switching, the status reads "Created release · tracks origin/release"; refs read back the same in both builds ([switched](../evidence/capped-menus-stashes-and-up-to-date/tracking-created-18pt-picker-check-no-band.png), [not switched](../evidence/capped-menus-stashes-and-up-to-date/tracking-created-no-switch-18pt.png)). Right-click menus open, dismiss, move to another row and replace one another identically in both builds; only a timing figure in the status bar differs. After one resize with no selection, `9ed2f45` still cuts the "No commits match" line mid-word and Clear search at the inspector 3 s later, while `a79b83c` lays out at 1,000 px by 0.3 s with both centred and whole ([3 s](../evidence/capped-menus-stashes-and-up-to-date/resize-3s-no-match-line-whole.png)). Shift+F10 and the Menu key on the navigator's "main" row open its menu with "Branch actions…" highlighted, and the author's tooltip at 461 × 490 reads "GitTurtle QA", identical in both builds. An idle window used 0–10 ms of CPU in 10 s in both builds (three runs each).
- **Review:** a read-only design review verified the claims above against the frames and checked them for privacy. **Defects, queued:** Escape on the tracking-branch review leaves 23 of the frame's 30 children "Unknown object" on the accessibility bus (21 of 28 at 461 × 490 18 pt; unchanged after 3 s; pre-existing in both builds, while confirming leaves none); a capped menu lights two rows after one wheel notch, the row last under the pointer keeping its fill ([two rows lit](../evidence/capped-menus-stashes-and-up-to-date/after-one-notch-13pt-two-rows-lit.png)); the keyboard menu's gap is 7.0 px at 18 pt (6.5 px at 13 pt), not the 8 px asked; the raised Changes row menu covers its own row by 5.5 px; at 18 pt in 461 px the key hints cut the outcome to "Up to date with ori…", "Created and switch…" and "Created release · tr…" ([cut](../evidence/capped-menus-stashes-and-up-to-date/fetch-nothing-new-18pt-status-cut.png)); the ahead and behind counts are cut to "↑ 0…"; at 1,000 × 680 18 pt the comparison placeholder pushes Restore staged state, Drop… and Review restore… below the fold until the detail scrolls (recommended: pin the actions as a footer at 1,000 × 680 and above, and keep the comparison at its placeholder's height until a file is chosen; [below the fold](../evidence/capped-menus-stashes-and-up-to-date/1000x680-18pt-detail-scrolls-actions-below-fold.png)); at 461 × 490 18 pt a scroll stop can cut Review restore… at the detail's top edge; and taste items (the empty Stashes list at 13 pt, the picker's tooltip over the path line, a one-item navigator menu, an author tooltip that repeats a whole label).
- **Limitations:** implementers were building and testing throughout (load 4–17), so this entry claims no motion or latency timing; the idle CPU figures are the only measurements. `scripts/native_qa/qa.py scenario run` and `display-check` were unavailable on this host: they drive XWayland/X11 through python-xlib, which this Hyprland host does not provide; the frames come from the session's Wayland driver instead. No screen reader was run.
- **Evidence:** [`docs/evidence/capped-menus-stashes-and-up-to-date/`](../evidence/capped-menus-stashes-and-up-to-date).

## October 28 night dialogs are announced as dialogs, row menus open from the keyboard, and narrow states keep their room

`d3c1622` gives plain dialogs the dialog role (alerts stay for write reviews and warnings), `e27b795` reads titles and lines by their text, `f0fe3d4` opens the Reflog, Worktrees and Tags with a ring on their first field, `dd922c9` caps every dialog above the status bar, `7cc1f13` keeps History search's state inside a narrow column, `cb7fa2e` keeps the narrow Stashes list's line inside it, `71ab8a4` opens History's and the trees' row menus from the keyboard, `71738a8` folds the navigation's modes, hint and placeholder before they wrap, `15f7b6c` reports only the drawn part of the Changed files rows, and `b75032c` ends the inspector's author inside its box; `d75fd70` redraws History once after a single resize.

- **Automated:** each commit's tests, failing with its fix removed; fast gate green at `9401e64`.
- **Host and capture:** Asahi Omarchy (Hyprland 0.56, native Wayland, scale 2), windows forced opaque, empty XDG homes per launch, accessibility bus on for the launches; the demo fixture tiled at 461 × 490 (13 and 18 pt, Midnight): eight dialogs from the palette with AT-SPI dumps, a search with no match, Stashes; the compact fixture tiled at 461 × 490 and floating at 1,480 × 980 and 1,480 × 800 (13 and 18 pt; the navigation at its default width and seeded to 180 px): the commit list's Shift+F10 and Menu key, F10, the Working Actions menu by pointer, a row's Menu key, the navigation and the author; the history-scroll fixture floating at 1,480 × 800, resized once to 1,000 × 800. Driver `run-q6.sh`; idle CPU with the accessibility bus off, `idle-cpu.sh`.
- **Builds:** base `9ed2f45` (sha256 `544a3606…`), candidate `a79b83c` (sha256 `87d8c9e1…`; product code `9eb7c8a`), both clean releases.
- **Results:** the Reflog, rebase review, Quick Open, Keyboard Shortcuts, Tags, Worktrees, the command palette and Stashes report the dialog role, named by their titles. Worktrees and Tags open with whole rings on their filters (none before). Keyboard Shortcuts ends at 438.5 px, 25.5 px above the status bar (it ran 11.5 px over it), with its selected entry whole. A search with no match keeps a 129 px list at 13 pt (145 px at 18 pt) with its line and Clear search inside it, where the list was 2.5 px and Clear search ended below the window; its line is 8.42:1. Shift+F10 on History's commit list opens the commit's menu under its row with "Revert this commit…" highlighted (nothing opened before); F10 and a row's Menu key open their menus on the first action, a pointer-opened menu on none; on a tracked file the row menu reads "Ignore…, untracked files only". At 18 pt in 1,480 × 800 Local, Remote and Worktrees become three 39 px icons on one row with tooltips, the filter reads "Filter branches…" and the keyboard hint gives way (the base wrapped "Worktrees", cut the placeholder and wrapped the hint to four lines); at 13 pt in a 180 px navigation the icons are 28 px. Changed files rows end inside their list on the bus (the base's ran past the window). After one resize with no input, an idle window used 0 ms of CPU in 10 s in both builds (three runs each).
- **Review:** three read-only design reviewers verified the claims above and checked the frames for privacy; crash notifications from a test probe appear in some full 1,480 px frames and in no crop. **Regressions found:** at 18 pt in 461 × 490 the Stashes dialog cuts its "Choose a stash…" line mid-line (the cap and the taller list together), and the keyboard commit menu ends 2 px above the status bar instead of 8. Also found: several dialog texts are still unnamed on the bus (Stashes' title and placeholder, the palette's title and hint, Quick Open's and Keyboard Shortcuts' hints, Projects' line); the shortened Shortcuts list shows no scrollbar; the commit menu's edge lands on the graph lane; the selected navigation mode is shown only by a 1.40:1 fill in icon form; the inspector title reports bounds past its frame. Not reached: the one-resize layout with no selection (the run had a commit selected, where the base already redraws), the navigator's row menu and the author's tooltip at 461 px (the driver's taps matched other nodes).
- **Evidence:** [`docs/evidence/dialogs-keyboard-menus-and-navigation-fold/`](../evidence/dialogs-keyboard-menus-and-navigation-fold).

## October 28 night every state line is read by name, and the compact bar keeps its edge whole or as a glyph

`9534e99` writes each empty and edge state's line through `native_accessibility::static_text`, so AT-SPI reads it by its whole line; `3914f34` keeps the compact bar's repository edge whole or turns it into its glyph, and shows a detached HEAD's short ID in the picker.

- **Automated:** `native_accessibility::state_tests` (three) and the width walk's new edge checks, failing with each fix removed; fast gate green at `3914f34`.
- **Host and capture:** Asahi Omarchy (Hyprland 0.56, native Wayland, scale 2), windows forced opaque, empty XDG homes per launch, accessibility bus on for the launches; fixtures from `tools/make-principles.sh` (local): no remote, no upstream, detached, no commits and a new `dirty` one (no remote, an edit and an untracked file), each floating at 1,480 × 980 in Midnight and tiled at 461 × 490, 18 pt, in Daylight: History (with an AT-SPI dump), Changes, a filter and a search with no match, Stashes from the Actions menu, Tags, the GitHub panel. Driver `run-pr.sh`, group e.
- **Builds:** parent `91fb95e` (sha256 `da676e6d…`), candidate `7342c3e` (sha256 `d3a153c0…`), both clean releases; `00f0add` (sha256 `1080743e…`) as a regression base for `91fb95e`.
- **Results:** History's dumps gain labels named "No remote" (described "This repository has no remote. Add one to fetch, pull and push."), "No upstream · Push sets one", "Detached at 0a7ea5b" and "No commits yet · Your first commit starts in Changes", in reading order between the branch picker and Fetch; the parent had none. At 18 pt in 461 px "No r…" becomes a 19.5 px remote glyph (6.70:1), and a detached HEAD shows "0a7ea5b" with the commit glyph in the picker (its name "Detached HEAD at 0a7ea5b") where "Detached HEAD" filled the bar to 3 px from Fetch and the line was not drawn. At 1,480 px nothing changes. From `00f0add` to `91fb95e` only the expected header, search, badge, narrow-dialog and GitHub panel changes appear.
- **Review:** a read-only design reviewer verified the claims above and checked the frames for privacy. Found, queued: the Working filter's "No matches" (first reached here) draws no line at 18 pt in 461 px and, at 1,480 px, sits above the Changes header and pushes it 21 px; Stashes' line overruns its list at 18 pt; a search with no match still leaves History's list 0 px tall at 18 pt (a fix is in flight); the detached compact bar shows two commit glyphs and never the word; the wide bar's detached picker pairs a branch glyph with "Detached HEAD". Not dumped natively: the state lines of Changes, Tags, Stashes and the GitHub panel (covered by `state_tests`).
- **Evidence:** [`docs/evidence/state-lines-and-compact-edge/`](../evidence/state-lines-and-compact-edge).

## October 28 night a write's outcome stays in the status line, Stage all confirms in view, and new history is cued in the toolbar

`ce5aaa6` holds a write's outcome in the status line for its 1.5 s, `8c3466e` brings Stage all's heading into view when it confirms, and `7342c3e` moves the new-commit notice from a full-width bar into History's scope toolbar.

- **Automated:** `confirmation_tests::the_status_line_keeps_a_writes_outcome_over_the_refresh_after_it`, `stage_all_brings_its_heading_into_view_beside_the_selected_file`, `history_updates::tests::new_history_is_cued_inside_the_scope_toolbar_and_moves_nothing`, each failing with its fix removed; fast gate green at `7342c3e`.
- **Host and capture:** Asahi Omarchy (Hyprland 0.56, native Wayland, scale 2), windows forced opaque, empty XDG homes per launch, accessibility bus on for the launches; the `pr/work` fixture (six edited files, two staged, an origin one commit ahead) floating at 1,480 × 980 in Midnight: a row's Stage and Unstage, Stage all, Fetch, Pull and Push, frames 0.2–0.7 s after each click and after its 1.5 s; tiled at 461 × 490, 18 pt: Fetch, then Fetch again with the pointer left on it (0.55 and 1.0 s). Driver `run-pr.sh`, group f, with the taps now matching Pull and Push by name.
- **Builds:** parent `91fb95e` (sha256 `da676e6d…`), candidate `7342c3e` (sha256 `d3a153c0…`), both clean releases; `00f0add` (sha256 `1080743e…`) as a regression base for `91fb95e`.
- **Results:** 0.2 s after each write the status line reads "Staged gamma.txt", "Unstaged alpha.txt" or "Staged all working changes" (the parent already showed the refreshed summary), and the summary returns after. Stage all's "Unstaged 0 · ✓ Staged" ends whole at the list's foot where the parent left it below the fold. After Fetch nothing moves (the parent's bar pushed History 35 px, 49 px at 18 pt in the half tile); "1 new commit" follows the count in the toolbar (15.67:1) and Latest carries a 6 px accent dot (8.5 px at 18 pt; 11.43:1), the dot alone at 461 px. Fetched, Pulled and Pushed confirm on their controls; the compact Fetch's "Fetched" tooltip shows within 0.55 s with the pointer on it.
- **Review:** a read-only design reviewer verified the claims above and checked the frames for privacy. Found, queued: Stage all's "✓ Staged" is drawn in the disabled colour (3.19:1) because its group is empty while it confirms; the cue and its dot stay through Pull and Push although the new commit is already on top; the dot sits 1.5 px from Latest's arrow at 18 pt; the compact Fetch tooltip covers "PRs". In the base builds Pulled and Pushed were missing from their controls 0.7 s after the click (the removed bar may have ended them); a test should pin that a history notice ends no confirmation. Not dumped: the cue's accessible name and its single announcement.
- **Evidence:** [`docs/evidence/feedback-outcome-and-new-commit-cue/`](../evidence/feedback-outcome-and-new-commit-cue).

Drivers `run-sp2.sh`, `run-sp2c.sh` and `run-all3-7342c3e.sh` (the polyglot at 18 pt and at 640 × 680).

## October 28 night commit details fit at 18 pt, and dialogs are named by their titles

- **Host and capture:** Asahi Omarchy (Hyprland 0.56, native Wayland, scale 2), windows forced opaque, empty XDG homes per launch, accessibility bus on for the launches; the compact fixture floating at 1,000 × 680 and 1,480 × 800, 13 and 18 pt, Midnight (History, Compare, Back hovered, Changes); the demo fixture at 1,480 × 980 in Midnight and Daylight with the Reflog and the interactive rebase review. Driver `run-q2.sh`, groups f and d.

The Reflog reads "Local reflog and recovery" and the rebase review "Edit local commits" on the bus where both read "Alert dialog"; both keep the alert role (queued). The Reflog opens with a whole ring on its filter (11.4:1 Midnight, 5.2:1 Daylight) and one Tab in the rebase form rings Load commits. History rows report only their drawn part (1,752 → 1,101 px wide at 18 pt in 1,000 px).
- **Review:** a read-only design reviewer verified the claims above and checked the frames for privacy. Found, queued: at 18 pt in 1,000 × 680 P1 ends 1 px above the details' clip, so its ring is likely cut (the focus sweep runs only at 1,280 × 800); the Reflog lists a row it does not draw (HEAD@{6}, over Done) and has no scroll affordance; at 18 pt in 1,480 × 800 the branch filter's placeholder is cut and Local/Remote/Worktrees wraps; the Midnight backdrop barely separates a dialog from History. Not reached: Back hovered at 18 pt (the pointer missed it), the loading row's race itself (the read had landed).

## October 28 Linux opens captured images, PDFs and Mermaid text in the default app, through the portal or xdg-open

`7df9ba8` names container signatures without a file name; `4c6acbc` offers Open in default app on Linux for content-decided passive types (raster images, PDF and plain text), writing an isolated read-only copy and handing it to the XDG Desktop Portal's OpenURI, or to `xdg-open` where no portal answers; `48660d6` offers no audio or video copies. Five independent security reviews preceded integration (the fifth passed; findings in [the plan's item 9](../development/omarchy-full-support.md#phases)).

- **Automated:** the commits' tests, each failing with its fix removed; fast gate green at `48660d6`.
- **Host and capture:** Asahi Omarchy on an Apple M2 Pro, Hyprland 0.56, native Wayland at scale 2, `xdg-desktop-portal` with the Hyprland and GTK backends; clean release builds; notifications silenced; windows forced opaque; empty XDG homes; controls reached by accessible name through AT-SPI. Fixture `/tmp/gitturtle-evidence/sysprev`, one added file per commit: a Mermaid file, Markdown, PNG, JPEG, AVIF, ICO, JPEG 2000, PDF, a `.txt`, a PNG carrying `<!DOCTYPE svg` (which GIO types as SVG), WAV and `.docx`. Floating 1,480 × 980 in Braden and Midnight for every row, tiles of 461 × 490 at 13 and 18 pt; a click per offered kind with the window list recorded before and after; then the same build on a private session bus with no portal. Steps `.local/item8-2026-10-27/tools/steps/sp.sh`, `run-sp.sh`, `run-sp-noportal.sh` (local).
- **Builds:** base `0aca11e` (sha256 `5b499cf9…`; no control on Linux), candidate `48660d6` (sha256 `7ef2d1c8…`).
- **Results:** the candidate offers the control on the After side of the Mermaid file's Diagrams view, every raster image (PNG, JPEG, AVIF, ICO, JPEG 2000, and the polyglot, which is a PNG by content) and the PDF; Markdown's Rendered view, the `.txt` diff, WAV and `.docx` offer nothing, and their comparison panes are pixel-identical to the base. The control is 28 logical px tall (39 at 18 pt). Through the portal, Mermaid text opened in Neovim in a terminal at once; PNG and JPEG opened GTK's "Open With…" chooser with Image Viewer preselected and then imv; AVIF the chooser and then Firefox; the PDF the chooser and then Evince; ICO and JPEG 2000 a chooser with no application, cancelled, which the app reported as nothing opened. The polyglot opened nothing: "The desktop would open this copy as image/svg+xml rather than as a PNG image, so nothing was opened." Every opened window named the copy "Captured revision.<suffix>", never the repository's path. Without a portal, the PNG opened imv and the PDF Evince through `xdg-open`, and the text was refused: "Text opens only through the desktop portal, which is not running here; xdg-open would choose an application from what the text contains, not its type."
- **Review:** a read-only design reviewer verified the above and checked the crops for privacy (fixture names only; the desktop bar is cropped out). Found, being fixed: the result is a window-wide banner with Dismiss that stays across files, and failures share the success tone; the image control's accessible name omits the type, and no control's name starts with its visible label; at 461 × 490 the control is cut at its column's edge at 13 and 18 pt (its test checks only the window's edge); the PDF control makes the After header 8.5 logical px taller than Before; the image control sits in its own row over the Before column; DESIGN.md, `docs/linux.md` and the README say plain text is offered where only Mermaid source is. Not shown: tooltips, hover, press and focus; Mermaid at 461 px; a changed side with both controls. Linux renders no PDF pages ("PDF pages are not rendered on this platform"): native page rendering is macOS's.
- **Evidence:** [`docs/evidence/linux-open-in-default-app/`](../evidence/linux-open-in-default-app).

Found, being fixed: Changes' code text still starts 10 logical px left of Source's (physical 1,042 against 1,062), since Source draws more room after its gutter than the commit modelled; "100 files per page · GitHub limit 3,000" starts at 415, off the panel's edge; in Files the path header and hint start at the box's edge while Changes, Source and Threads start about 10 logical px in. ## October 28 narrow History: a whole ring, whole lanes, named references and tags

`7294b63` draws History's ring around the list the viewport shows; `1b1a878` names every row's references; `7f5c7b1` shows the selected commit's lane after History narrows; `1832fa2` draws narrow History's graph in whole lanes; `45547f7` shows tags in History's references; `d0a9836` corrects the floor figures in DESIGN.md.

- **Automated:** the commits' tests, each failing with its fix removed; fast gate green at `48660d6`. The tag read's release measurement is [its own record](../benchmarks/2026-10-28-history-snapshot-tags.md).
- **Host and capture:** as the menus entry above; tiles of 461 × 490 and 640 × 680 at 13 and 18 pt in Midnight and Braden and a floating 1,480 × 800 at 13 pt on the `demo` fixture (tag `v0.1-demo` on its root commit); hovers on the references pill at the centres measured last session. Steps `.local/item8-2026-10-27/tools/steps/q1-nh.sh` (local).
- **Builds:** base `0aca11e` (sha256 `5b499cf9…`), candidate `48660d6` (sha256 `7ef2d1c8…`).
- **Results:** the base's list ring had only its corner pixels on the right in all eight tiles; the candidate draws a full 2 px right side in each (11.43:1 Midnight, 5.23:1 Braden), and it stays while the pointer moves. At 1,480 px four rows now name their references ("… · References: main, release/0.1, review/accessibility, origin/main · …"; none in the base). At 461 × 490, 13 pt the base cut third-lane nodes in half at the column's edge; the candidate draws two whole lanes and nothing cut. The root commit shows a `v0.1-demo` chip with its tag glyph in the wide References column and in the 640 × 680 badge (label 8.88:1 Midnight, 6.31:1 Braden), named "References: tag v0.1-demo". The pill's tooltip, owed since the earlier reviews, lists every reference at 461 × 490 and 640 × 680 (unchanged from the base).
- **Review:** a read-only design reviewer verified the claims above and checked the frames for privacy. Found: DESIGN.md says a 461 px window at the default size shows one lane, where Linux draws two; at 18 pt the graph now drops a third lane that the base drew whole, for about one character of subject (to be decided by the whole-lane rule); History rows report AT-SPI bounds past the window (being fixed). A second capture (`steps/q1-nh4.sh`) reached the rest: widened to 1,400 × 490 with a third-lane commit selected and narrowed back to 461 × 490, the base cut the selected node in half at the column's edge while the candidate scrolls the lanes so it is whole, 8 px inside the column (`7f5c7b1`); the root row's compact pill leads with the tag glyph and its tooltip reads "tag v0.1-demo" at 13 and 18 pt in both palettes; the pill alone carries the tooltip (a hover on the subject shows none, as in the base).
- **Evidence:** [`docs/evidence/narrow-history-lanes-and-tags/`](../evidence/narrow-history-lanes-and-tags).

`d1299fb` (`narrow-large-text-compact-chrome`): below a measured height the header and the action bar take a compact form (icon toggles, a compact repository heading, a one-row action bar), so a 461 × 490 half tile at 18 pt keeps History rows and diff lines; only History's need set the breakpoint (Compare's was added in `adc70bd`). The composer heading stays whole in a short window.

- **Automated:** `chrome_prediction_matches_the_laid_out_header_and_action_bar` (72 cases), `short_large_text_keeps_history_rows_and_diff_lines`, `compact_chrome_keeps_each_control`, `chrome_tab_order` and `composer_heading_stays_whole_in_a_short_window`, each failing with its fix removed. Fast gate green at `d1299fb`.
- **Host and capture:** Asahi Omarchy on an Apple M2 Pro, Hyprland 0.56, native Wayland at scale 2; release builds; notifications silenced; windows forced opaque; empty XDG homes. Tiles of 461 × 490 at 13 and 18 pt in Midnight and Braden, 493 × 526 at 13 pt, and floating 1,000 × 680 and 1,480 × 800 at 13 pt; `compact` fixture. Steps `.local/item8-2026-10-27/tools/steps/compact.sh` (local).
- **Builds:** base `0dd91c0` (sha256 `d8415e14…`, product code of `c7ad38f`), candidate `d1299fb` (sha256 `90743392…`).
- **Results:** at 13 pt every frame is pixel-identical to the base except the 461 × 490 Changes composer, whose heading is now pinned whole; at 18 pt in 461 × 490 the header and action bar compact, History shows rows and Compare shows diff lines where the base showed chrome. History scrolling costs no measurable UI-thread time: [the scroll record](../benchmarks/2026-10-27-compact-chrome-history-scroll.md) reads Δp50 −0.09 ms [−1.04, +0.66] at 461 × 490 and, rerun on a quiet host, −1.07 ms [−2.05, −0.09] at 1,480 × 800 (a first run beside worktree builds had read +1.27 ms, a confound).
- **Review:** a read-only design reviewer checked the frames for privacy (fixture data only). Its findings (no file row in the short Working inspector, hidden title field and actions, a Changes toggle without its count, toggles moving between modes, lone ellipses in inspector rows, a clipped Compare footer, wrapped mode toggles, the subject dropped from narrow History, a different profile icon, a cramped 13 pt composer and uneven insets) were fixed in `208676a`, `adc70bd`, `61653f0`, `f5e70d3` and their follow-ups, recorded in the entries above.
- **Evidence:** [`docs/evidence/narrow-large-text-compact-chrome/`](../evidence/narrow-large-text-compact-chrome), `{base,candidate}-{midnight,daylight}-18pt-461x490-{history,compare,changes}.png` and the 13 pt composer.

At 461 × 490 Options ends the review row, and the menu ends with a separator and "Original Git diff", which Up and Down skip. The narrow Reflog runs 24 px from each edge with entries starting at its padding; at 1,480 the dialog keeps 740 px and entries are left-aligned.
- **Review:** a read-only design reviewer confirmed each change and checked the frames for privacy (fixture data only), and found: the caption in the menu draws in the kit's disabled colour (2.97:1 Midnight, 2.19:1 Braden) and AT-SPI announces it as a menu item; the narrow item's accessible name differs from the wide button's; `docs/native-accessibility.md` still says "Context"; the Reflog's tooltip appears even when nothing is cut; its header, entries and filter start 776, 792 and 798 px. Queued in item 9.
- **Evidence:** [`docs/evidence/review-context-caption-reflog/`](../evidence/review-context-caption-reflog), `{base,candidate}-{midnight,daylight}-{context-wide,options-menu-narrow,review-row-narrow,reflog-wide,reflog-hover-wide,reflog-narrow}.png`.

## October 27 copy actions say Copied where they were clicked

Approved item 8 of [full Omarchy support](../development/omarchy-full-support.md), task `copy-actions-say-copied`, `2133809`: a copy action now confirms itself on its own button, a check and "Copied" for 1.5 s in the button's reserved width, and a polite status announces what was copied (`copy_feedback.rs`).

- **Host and capture:** Asahi Omarchy on an Apple M2 Pro, Hyprland 0.56, native Wayland at scale 2; release builds; notifications silenced; Midnight and Braden at 13 pt, floating 1,480 × 980, forced opaque; empty XDG homes; controls clicked by accessible name through AT-SPI. The disposable `wording` fixture of [the wording entry](#october-27-a-linux-build-names-linux-keys-and-offers-no-mac-only-action). Steps: History on the newest commit, the inspector's Hash, Copy message, then Compare on the recoloured `orbit.svg` and Copy After source; each copied state shot about 0.8 s after its click and again 2 s later. Driver `.local/item8-2026-10-27/tools/float.sh` with `steps/copy.sh` (local).

- **Results:** frames differ only at the copy buttons and the status bar's timing. Just after a click the candidate's Hash, Copy message and Copy After source read a check and "Copied" in the label colour (about 14.5:1 in Midnight, 12.9:1 in Braden) without changing width, and return to rest by 2 s; the base never changes. AT-SPI: the buttons keep their names ("Copy full commit hash"), and a status node announces "Copied commit hash", "Copied commit message" and "Copied After source"; the base announces nothing. At rest the Hash button is 48 → 80 px wide (AT-SPI; the implementer's 86 px came from the test harness's font), its label centred in the reserved width.
- **Review:** a read-only design reviewer confirmed each step and found: the centred content leaves a 40 px gap before Hash at rest and makes Copy message and Copy After source jump right when copied (start-aligning the cell keeps both in place); "Copied" follows a fixed button id, so another commit selected within 1.5 s reads Copied; Hash may wrap at 18 pt in a half tile; announcements from dialogs come from the window root, unchecked with Orca. Queued in item 9, with frames still owed of a copy while hovered, pressed or activated by keyboard, an icon-only copy button and a repeated click. Privacy: all frames show fixture data only.
