## October 27 a Linux build names Linux keys and offers no Mac-only action

Item 9 of [full Omarchy support](../development/omarchy-full-support.md), the Linux wording sweep (owner decision: first), `5ce690b` and `69dc3de`. A Linux build named macOS keys, places and apps: the conflict viewer's tooltips said Command-Option-Up/Down while the viewer listened for Super with Alt, which Hyprland owns; Compare's change arrows said Option/Alt-Up; the rebase help said Option-↑/↓; hints and an accessible description said Return; the PR review said "Drafts stay on this Mac", Shift-Up/Down and Return; GitHub CLI guidance said "In Terminal"; PDF and AVIF/HEIF/JPEG 2000 previews said the format "requires macOS" and told the user to export the captured bytes; and the image, PDF, model and document previews offered a System preview that on Linux could only fail with a Quick Look error. Shortcut text now comes from `shortcuts.rs` (`key_label`, `shortcuts::ENTER`, `shortcuts::SHIFT_CLICK`), the conflict viewer steps blocks with the primary modifier its tooltips name, and `rich_preview::SYSTEM_PREVIEW` leaves System preview, and the GIF sentence that pointed to it, to macOS ([file previews](../file-previews.md#support-matrix), [conflict blocks](../conflict-blocks.md)).

- **Automated:** `conflicts::tests::the_advertised_block_keys_step_between_blocks` parses the keys the tooltips name and asserts they step blocks, that Super with Alt does not on Linux, and that the Linux label is "Ctrl+Alt+Down"; it fails with the old Super check. The preview crate's JPEG 2000 test asserts the new platform message. Fast gate green at both commits.
- **Host and capture:** Asahi Omarchy on an Apple M2 Pro, Hyprland 0.56, native Wayland at scale 2; release builds; notifications silenced; Midnight at 13 pt, floating 1,480 × 980 and forced opaque; empty XDG homes per launch; controls clicked by accessible name through AT-SPI, enabled only for these launches. Disposable fixtures by the QA identity under `/tmp/gitturtle-evidence/`: `wording` (a PDF, an STL, a WAV, a ZIP and an SVG that a later commit recolours; two text commits), `wording-conflict` (a merge left with two conflicting blocks), `wording-gif` (a 160-frame GIF past the 120-frame cap) and `wording-work` (two unstaged edits), plus the offline review fixture. Driver `.local/item8-2026-10-27/tools/float.sh` with `steps/wording-*.sh` (local).
- **Builds:** base `b573876` (the sweep's parent, product code equal to `2133809`; clean tree, sha256 `2328c355…`), candidate `69dc3de` (both commits; the only uncommitted file was `docs/development/HANDOFF.md`; sha256 `b13baf12…`). The first pass, base `2133809` and candidate `5ce690b`, showed the same differences.
- **Results:** every frame pair differs only where the text changed, and where a removed System preview row lets the content below it move up (the ZIP, WAV and PDF After headers now line up with Before). The candidate reads "Enter Open file" in History's status bar, "Enter opens" in the palette and Quick Open, "Next change · Alt+Down" and "Previous change · Alt+Up", "Alt+Up / Alt+Down reorder", "Next conflict · Ctrl+Alt+Down", "Drafts stay on this computer", "Shift+click or Shift+Up / Shift+Down … Enter comments" and the same keys in the selection error, "Shift+click selects a range", "Shift+F10 actions", "PDF pages are not rendered on this platform." and the GIF limit without the System preview sentence. AT-SPI: the SVG offers Copy Before and Copy After source in both builds and System preview only in the base; the PR file list's description says "Enter moves to its patch." After Ctrl+Alt+Down in the conflict viewer the candidate's block status reads "selected 2 · result line 39"; the base stays at "selected 1 · result line 20".
- **Review:** a read-only design reviewer checked every pair, found the PDF and codec messages, the review error, "Shift-click" and "Shift F10" left after `5ce690b` (fixed in `69dc3de`), kept "Esc" in the status bar's key-cap hints beside "Ctrl+F", and viewed every committed frame at full resolution for privacy: fixture data only. Open: the rebase dialog's keyboard help is not on the accessibility bus in either build; "In a terminal, run ‘gh auth login …’" appears only when the GitHub CLI cannot be started, so it has no native frame.
- **Evidence:** [`docs/evidence/linux-wording/`](../evidence/linux-wording), `base-*.png` and `candidate-*.png` crops of each string.

## October 27 light palettes' hover stands off subtle, and pressed ghost buttons take selected

Approved item 8 of [full Omarchy support](../development/omarchy-full-support.md), task `builtin-light-hover-lift` (`63414e3`), and a follow-up from the ghost-hover design review (`c7ad38f`).

- **Hover off subtle:** secondary controls (the branch and profile pickers, Fetch, Pull) rest on `subtle` and hover to `hover`, and the Omarchy fitter keeps that step at 1.08:1. Five light built-ins sat closer; each hover is darkened in linear light, keeping its hue: Braden (`daylight`) 1.047 → 1.086, Porcelain 1.076 → 1.086, One Light 1.010 → 1.084 (its hover had been lighter than `subtle`), Rosé Pine Dawn 1.032 → 1.090, Alucard 1.056 → 1.087. `appearance::tests::every_built_in_hover_steps_off_subtle` holds all twenty built-ins to the rule and failed on the five; the focus-ring, pressed-step, readability and Omarchy mapping tests pass unchanged.
- **Pressed ghost:** a pressed ghost Button, and a selected one, took the kit's `secondary` lightened or darkened by 20% at 80%, which in Midnight sat under the hover. The kit takes a code-set `Theme::ghost_active`, set to `Palette::control_fill(selected)`; `views::tests::pressed_ghost_buttons_take_the_selected_layer` holds the narrow review arrow down in Midnight and Daylight and fails without the app's setting.
- **Host and capture:** as in [the ghost-hover entry](#october-27-ghost-buttons-lift-on-hover-and-ticked-menu-items-are-check-items). Hover: the review fixture with an origin (so Fetch is enabled), floating 1,480 × 980, 13 pt, History at rest and with the branch picker, Fetch and the profile picker hovered, in the five palettes; base `f57a514`, candidate `63414e3` (sha256 `e34544cf…`). Pressed: narrow Compare at 461 × 490, the previous arrow held down, Midnight and Daylight; base `63414e3`, candidate `c7ad38f` (sha256 `8663b3b3…`, built in its own target directory).
- **Results:** rest → hover at each control, base 1.010–1.077, candidate 1.078–1.091 (One Light's Fetch lowest; the capture shifts colours by up to three steps, probably Omarchy's 0.985 window opacity). Rest frames differ only at the "Changed files" count chip, drawn on `hover`, whose number reads 7.50–13.40:1. Pressed arrow: Midnight #1a202e (1.010:1 on the panel, under its hover) → #243b3c, Daylight #bbc8e0 → #daeae5, each within two steps of `selected`.
- **Review:** a read-only design reviewer accepted both on contract and confirmed every number. Hover against pressed: Porcelain, Rosé Pine Dawn and Alucard keep hover lighter; in Braden they now have the same brightness and differ by hue (as Kanagawa Wave's do), and in One Light pressed is lighter than hover; Rosé Pine Dawn's pressed step is exactly the minimum of 6 (Braden 8, Porcelain 7). DESIGN.md and the themes spec are corrected to match. Taste: pressing a secondary control in Braden and One Light reads as a colour change rather than a deeper press; a slightly darker `selected` in Braden and Rosé Pine Dawn would restore margin. Privacy: all 14 committed files show fixture data only; the desktop wallpaper bleeds through the 0.985 opacity at a level visible only under contrast enhancement.
- **Open:** frames of a hovered row beside the selected row in Braden and One Light, and of pressed Fetch in Braden and Rosé Pine Dawn; the Reflog's current entry under `c7ad38f`. Committed frames this moves were not retaken: those showing these palettes' hover or a count chip (for example `review-arrow-buttons-hover/*-daylight-*`, `tab-lists-scroll-cues/*porcelain*-hover*`, `button-states/porcelain-*`) and `reflog-selected/`; every frame made before `ad04939` already differs from the current build in its font.
- **Evidence:** [`docs/evidence/builtin-light-hover-lift/`](../evidence/builtin-light-hover-lift), `{base,candidate}-{braden,porcelain,one-light,rose-pine-dawn,alucard}-pickers.png` (rows: rest, branch picker, Fetch, profile picker hovered; columns: branch picker, Fetch and Pull, profile picker, count chip); [`docs/evidence/ghost-button-pressed/`](../evidence/ghost-button-pressed), `{base,candidate}-{midnight,daylight}-previous-pressed.png`.

## October 27 ghost buttons lift on hover, and ticked menu items are check items

Approved item 8 of [full Omarchy support](../development/omarchy-full-support.md), tasks `review-arrow-buttons-hover` (`2ab3348`) and `menu-checked-items-accessible` (`f57a514`).

- **Hover cause and fix:** the vendored kit filled a hovered ghost Button with `secondary` lightened by 10% at 80% opacity, and its `lighten` scales lightness, so over the app's dark `subtle` the fill barely moved (in Midnight it was two steps darker than the rest). The kit now takes a code-set `Theme::ghost_hover`, which the app sets to the shared helper's hover layer, `Palette::control_fill(hover)` ([patch note](../../vendor/gpui-component/GITTURTLE-PATCH.md), DESIGN.md's shared control contract). Every ghost Button changes: Compare's review arrows, Options, Hide whitespace, Context and Reset review, editor Find, the branch, tag, reflog, recovery, worktree, project and project-pane controls that call `.ghost()`, and the kit's own ghost controls.
- **Menu cause and fix:** the kit drew a checked item's tick only as an icon and gave every item `Role::MenuItem`. A checkable item (`PopupMenuItem::checked`, the `menu_with_check*` builders) is now `Role::MenuItemCheckBox` with its toggled state, and a plain item keeps `Role::MenuItem`. The review Options menu's Hide whitespace is checkable while Context and Reset review stay plain; the branch chooser's other branches take `.checked(false)` beside the ticked current one ([native accessibility](../native-accessibility.md#toolkit-patch)).
- **Automated:** `views::tests::narrow_review_arrows_lift_on_hover` hovers the narrow review arrows in Midnight and Daylight and reads the painted quads: no fill at rest, exactly the shared hover layer when hovered, none when an operation disables them; it fails without the app's `ghost_hover`. `views::tests::review_options_menu_announces_its_toggle_checked` opens the real Options menu and reads each item's role and state from a debug selector the kit names with the values it gives AccessKit, since GPUI's test platform never activates accessibility; it fails with the kit handing items no check state. Fast gate green at both commits.
- **Host and capture:** Asahi Omarchy on an Apple M2 Pro, Hyprland 0.56, native Wayland at scale 2; release builds; notifications silenced. A disposable fixture, `/tmp/gitturtle-evidence/compact` (40 commits by the QA identity, two staged files and one modified), empty XDG homes, 13 pt, the app tiled beside and above `foot` fillers and resized to exactly 461 × 490 (`.local/item8-2026-10-27/tools/tile.sh`, local). Return opens Compare; the pointer rests off the row, then over the previous arrow, the next arrow and Options.
- **Builds:** base `ff7fc3c` (sha256 `796a645c…`), the product code before both commits; candidate `f57a514` (sha256 `d4c79c9c…`), which carries both.
- **Hover results:** rest frames are byte-identical between builds. Hovered fills over the row's #1a1f2d rest in Midnight: base #181e2b, candidate #242c3d (about 1.19:1); in Daylight over #fefcfd: base #d6ddec, a kit grey darker than Daylight's `selected`, candidate #e7eaf2 (about 1.18:1), which is the palette's hover composited as the shared helper draws it. The capture path shifts absolute values by up to three steps; differences between frames are exact.
- **AT-SPI results:** `org.a11y.Status IsEnabled` was `false`, set `true` for the launches and restored to `false`, read back. With the Options menu open (`.local/item8-2026-10-27/tools/atspi_menu.py`, local): base reads Hide whitespace, Context 3 + and Reset review as `menu item`, the same ticked or not; candidate reads Hide whitespace as `check menu item`, `checkable`, and `checkable`, `checked` once ticked, and the other two as `menu item`. The open menu draws identically in both builds.
- **Review:** a read-only design reviewer confirmed the fills, that the candidate's hover equals the shared helper's layer (Midnight's matches the helper's hover in `button-states/`), that the lighter Daylight hover restores hover below pressed, and the AT-SPI readings; it viewed every committed file at full resolution: fixture data only. Found: a pressed or toggled ghost Button still takes the kit's derived fill (`ghost_active`, followed up); in every hovered frame the changed-file list's focus ring hides while the pointer moves (both builds); taste, "Context 3 +" reads without a verb, and the narrow caption truncates to "Origin…".
- **Not covered:** the kit's own ghost controls (dialog close, input clear, Find) were not captured; the layer is the same on any surface by construction. Disabled menu items read `enabled` over AT-SPI in both builds (the kit's menu item sets no disabled flag), reported for its own fix. macOS VoiceOver and Orca speech were not exercised.
- **Evidence:** [`docs/evidence/review-arrow-buttons-hover/`](../evidence/review-arrow-buttons-hover), `{base,candidate}-{midnight,daylight}-{rest,previous-hover,next-hover,options-hover}.png`, the review row of each frame; [`docs/evidence/menu-checked-items-accessible/`](../evidence/menu-checked-items-accessible), the ticked menu and the four AT-SPI readings.

## October 27 PR review files prepare without a copy and resume on reopen

Approved item 8 of [full Omarchy support](../development/omarchy-full-support.md), task `review-file-preparation-lifecycle`, `ff7fc3c`: selecting a file in a pull request's Files list copied the whole `PullFile`, patch included, on the UI thread before its background preparation; the patch is now an `Arc<str>` the preparation shares. Closing the panel cancels a pending preparation, and reopening reselected the file only in Changes, so a file left in Source reopened as plain text; the close now remembers a pending preparation and the reopen resumes it in whichever mode the file was left ([GitHub collaboration](../github-collaboration.md)).

- **Automated:** `github_view::review::tests::selecting_a_review_file_shares_its_patch_with_the_preparation` selects a file with an 8,336,022-byte patch and asserts the prepared file's patch is the view's allocation; `github_view::review::tests::reopening_resumes_an_interrupted_file_preparation` closes the panel while the selected file prepares, reopens it through `open_github` and asserts the rows and presentation arrive in Changes, and in Source the same editor takes the decorations Compare draws for the same text, and that a preparation which failed (a refused path) is not repeated. Both fail on `30b8868`'s product code and with their fix removed (a deep copy of the patch; the old reopen condition; a close that always resumes); `warm_reopen_retries_interrupted_local_read_and_template_payload_evicts` passes. Fast gate green.
- **Performance:** the selection's UI-thread time with two 8.3 MB patches, release, 180 samples per build: after a prepared file p50 0.582 → 0.216 ms, p95 0.929 → 0.232 ms, max 1.393 → 0.261 ms; while the previous file still prepares p50 0.674 → under 0.001 ms ([record](../benchmarks/2026-10-27-review-file-selection.md)).
- **Host and capture:** Asahi Omarchy on an Apple M2 Pro, Hyprland 0.56, native Wayland at scale 2; release builds; notifications silenced. The offline review fixture (`GITTURTLE_GITHUB_FIXTURE=review`) on a disposable repository, `/tmp/gitturtle-evidence/review`, whose origin names `gitturtle-fixture/native-review`; empty XDG config, data, state and cache homes; Midnight at 13 pt, floating 1,480 × 980. Route: the palette's "GitHub pull requests", Refresh fixture PRs, #42, Files · 4, Return selects `src/review/session.rs`, Source, Escape closes the panel, the palette reopens it. Driver `.local/item8-2026-10-27/tools/review.sh` (local).
- **Builds:** base `278b822`, the parent's product code (`30b8868` changed only documentation), not `origin/main`, whose font and fixes differ everywhere; candidate `ff7fc3c` (sha256 `24fb5a29…`, `.local/item8-2026-10-27/gitturtle-ff7fc3c.sha256`).
- **Results:** base and candidate frames are pixel-identical at every step, and Source after reopening shows the same decorated patch as before closing: old and new line numbers, the removed and added tints with their changed-word bands, and blue hunk headers. The fixture's patch prepares in microseconds, so a close cannot interrupt it natively; this pass shows the reopen route unchanged, and the interruption itself is covered by the view test above.
- **Review:** a read-only design reviewer confirmed the pixel identity, the decorations after reopening, and viewed every frame at full resolution for privacy: fixture data only. Found, pre-existing and identical in both builds: the panel's footer says "Drafts stay on this Mac" on Linux; after Escape closes the panel no control shows focus (to probe); Source ends half a row above the file list's bottom with empty dialog below; the status bar keeps History's hints under the dialog.
- **Evidence:** [`docs/evidence/review-file-preparation-lifecycle/`](../evidence/review-file-preparation-lifecycle), `candidate-midnight-source-{before-close,after-reopen}.png`, crops of the dialog.

## October 27 diff fences in Markdown source take the patch colours

Approved item 8 of [full Omarchy support](../development/omarchy-full-support.md), task `markdown-diff-fence-colours`: an editor showing Markdown source injects the diff grammar into ```` ```diff ```` fences, whose upstream query drew removed lines and `---` in the theme's keyword colour (`renamed`, violet), `+++` in its string colour, `@@` lines in its accent and context lines in the fence's code colour. `fd551c6` bundles that query in the vendored highlighter with the lines captured as names of their own (`diff.header`, `diff.hunk`, `diff.minus`, `diff.plus`), which the app draws in `text`, the fitted `hunk`, a newly fitted `removed` and `added`; `278b822` adds `diff.context` in `text`, after the design review saw context lines in the same green as added ones ([themes spec](../development/themes/spec.md#diff-and-syntax-colors), [patch note](../../vendor/gpui-component/GITTURTLE-PATCH.md)).

- **Automated:** `appearance::tests::a_diff_fence_in_markdown_draws_the_patch_colours` highlights a Markdown document with a fence in every built-in and embedded Omarchy palette and asserts each fence line's colour and its 4.5:1 on the canvas and the panel, and that Markdown with a Rust fence and no diff fence takes no style from the diff fields; it fails with the upstream query (Midnight's `---` draws in the keyword violet) and without the context capture. The selection, Find and changed-word highlights of all 44 test palettes are identical before and after (the word tints leave the `removed` role out, `SyntaxRoles::word_colors`). Fast gate green at `fd551c6` and `278b822`.
- **Host and capture:** as in [the Settings entry](#october-27-settings-keeps-keyboard-focus-in-view-and-switches-still); a disposable fixture, `/tmp/gitturtle-evidence/fence`, two commits by the QA identity, the second adding a `NOTES.md` section with a fence (headers, a hunk, two context lines, one removed and one added line); floating 1,480 × 980; History, Enter on the newest commit's file, Compare's **After**, then a click on the removed line; Midnight, Braden and the Omarchy theme (the desktop's Tokyo Night). Driver `.local/item8-2026-10-27/tools/fence.sh` (local).
- **Builds:** base `959fe6b`, the parent's product code, not `origin/main` (font and fixes differ everywhere); candidate `278b822` (sha256 in `.local/item8-2026-10-27/gitturtle-278b822.sha256`).
- **Results:** base and candidate differ only on the fence's header, hunk, context and removed lines (and the status bar's per-launch timing, outside the crops). Most common glyph colour, base → candidate: Midnight `---` #c1a4f3 and `+++` #77deba → #e8ecf5, `@@` #75ddba → #95b8fd, context #75ddba → #e6ebf5, removed #c0a3f3 → #fd93a8; Braden #7a50b3 and #176745 → #283348, `@@` #0a735e → #3863ad, context #156645 → #263248, removed #784fb3 → #b43352; Tokyo Night #ae8ee5 and #9fcc6a → #aab0d5, context #9dcc6b → #a8afd5, removed #ac8de5 → #f5758e, and `@@` unchanged, its hunk being its accent. On the active line the removed line keeps its colour (#fd93a8 on #1a202c, #b43352 on #fdfcfd, #f5758e on #16161d). The added line is unchanged.
- **Review:** a read-only design reviewer viewed all 12 committed crops at full resolution: only the fixture's text, line numbers and the editor background, no metadata, no identity. It confirmed the colour changes to within one channel step and that nothing else differs. Measured on the canvas: headers 15.3, 11.6 and 7.9:1 (Midnight, Braden, Tokyo Night), `@@` 9.1, 5.4 and 6.6:1, removed 8.6, 5.4 and 6.3:1, context 15.2, 11.6 and 7.9:1, and on the active line removed 7.9, 5.8 and 6.8:1. Its first pass found the context lines drawn in the added green, which `278b822` fixed, and asked for an active-line frame, now included. Taste: in Tokyo Night the `@@` line is the heading's blue, as the spec follows (its hunk is its accent).
- **Open:** no frame puts a fence line under a selection or Find match (the unit tests cover them); a `.diff` file shown as source takes the same colours, not captured; macOS.
- **Evidence:** [`docs/evidence/markdown-diff-fence-colours/`](../evidence/markdown-diff-fence-colours), `{base,candidate}-{midnight,braden,omarchy-tokyo-night}-{after,active-line}.png`, crops of the After editor's first sixteen lines.

## October 27 Settings keeps keyboard focus in view and switches still

Approved item 8 of [full Omarchy support](../development/omarchy-full-support.md), task `settings-focus-reveal-and-steady-switch`: Tab and Shift-Tab moved focus onto Settings controls outside the page's viewport without scrolling them in (only Your themes rows were revealed), and at 18 pt toggling **Use the desktop's monospace font** moved its Switch 11 px. Cause of the move: the row centres the Switch beside a description that changes with the setting, and the off text wraps to one more line than the pending text and the answer, so the row's height changed and the Switch and every row below moved. `959fe6b` makes the page's scroll a keyboard-triggered `FocusReveal` that every card, switch, button, checkbox and field registers with, through new caller focus handles on the vendored Switch and Checkbox ([patch note](../../vendor/gpui-component/GITTURTLE-PATCH.md)), and lays the row's off and pending texts invisibly in the description's grid cell, so the row keeps the height of the tallest.

- **Automated:** `settings::picker_tests::tab_keeps_every_settings_control_in_view` and `tab_keeps_the_identity_form_in_view` tab through the whole page in a 1,000 × 680 window, without and with a repository, then Shift-Tab back through the same controls; in every frame painted after each key the focused control with the ring's 3 px lies inside the viewport, and the walk scrolls past the first screen and back. Both fail with the in-frame reveal removed (frame 0 after a Tab draws a card outside the page) and with nothing registered, as on `origin/main`. `toggling_a_settings_switch_moves_nothing` toggles Follow system, Restore repository tabs and the code font Switch (off, pending, found, unavailable) at 13 pt × 0.875, 1 and 1.25 of the desktop scale and at 18 pt in a 1,000 px window, and every Switch track and row keeps its bounds; with the reserved texts removed it fails at the first size (the track moves 7.5 px, the next row 14.5 px). Show the project list is left out: narrowing the page for its pane is what it does. Fast gate green at `959fe6b`.
- **Host and capture:** Asahi Omarchy 4.0.4-mac.1, Hyprland 0.56, native Wayland, scale 2; Midnight; the demo fixture with an empty `XDG_CONFIG_HOME` (fontconfig linked); notifications silenced. Driver `.local/item8-2026-10-27/tools/reveal.sh` (local). (A) 13 pt, floating 1,000 × 680: Settings from the palette, 16 Tabs, then 12 Shift-Tabs, a frame after each. (B) 18 pt, 1,480 × 980, desktop font off: two Page Downs, then a click on the code font Switch and a second click at the same point.
- **Builds:** base `4401009`, the change's parent's product code (`.local/interface-font-2026-10-27/gitturtle-4401009`), not `origin/main`, whose interface font and fixes differ on every screen; candidate `959fe6b` (sha256 `08f74188b54e8dcd…`).
- **Results:** (A) Both builds are pixel-identical through the 11th Tab and at rest. The 12th Tab focuses a theme card that the viewport's lower edge cuts: the base page does not move, so the card's ring shows only its top and sides; the candidate scrolls 84.5 px, which leaves the ring's outer edge flush with the status bar, the least that shows it whole. By the 15th Tab the base's focus is wholly below the edge and no ring is visible, while the candidate has scrolled a further 138.5 px. On the way back the candidate scrolls up 16 px and 71.5 px at the 8th and 9th Shift-Tab, its ring then flush with the header. Shifts were measured as the least mean difference between consecutive frames (`tools/scroll.py`). (B) On the base, turning the Switch on moves the rows below up 23 px and the Switch up under the pointer, so the second click lands below it and it stays on. On the candidate the rows move 0 px, the only changes are the row's own text and the code sample above it, which switches to the desktop font, and the second click turns it off; that frame differs from rest only by the click's focus ring around the track.
- **Review:** a read-only design reviewer viewed all 13 committed frames at full resolution: they show only the app, the fixture's "demo" tab, built-in palette names and the desktop font's name (JetBrainsMono Nerd Font), no private data, no notification. The ring is whole on every side, 1 px gap and 2 px ring, and nothing is clipped; the reserved line under a one-line description reads as deliberate spacing (a matter of taste). It corrected two claims of the first draft, the 12th Tab's scroll (84.5 px, not 310) and the base's partly visible ring at that Tab, both fixed above, and confirmed the others. Not from this change: after the second click the candidate's track keeps the hover lift and the pointer is drawn, as on the parent.
- **Open:** no frame shows the pending text or a click on a partly visible control scrolling nothing (the tests cover the click); macOS and fractional scale factors.
- **Evidence:** [`docs/evidence/settings-focus-reveal-and-steady-switch/`](../evidence/settings-focus-reveal-and-steady-switch): `both-13pt-tab-11`, `{base,candidate}-13pt-{tab-12,tab-15,back-09}` (full windows) and `{base,candidate}-18pt-switch-{rest,on,off}` (crops of the code font row and the rows below).

## October 27 switches show focus and hover

Approved item 8 of [full Omarchy support](../development/omarchy-full-support.md), task `switch-focus-ring-and-hover`: a Tab onto a Settings switch showed nothing and hovering one changed nothing. `4401009` draws the Button focus ring around a focused enabled Switch's track and lifts an enabled unchecked track on hover by the palette's row step (`Palette::switch_hover`). `settings::picker_tests::a_focused_switch_draws_the_button_ring_whole_and_moves_nothing` tabs through the real Settings page at 13 and 18 pt and reads the scene: before the change no switch drew a ring; after it every enabled switch draws one, whole, and no other quad moves. `appearance::tests::every_palette_lifts_a_hovered_switch_track` holds the 6/255 minimum in every built-in and Omarchy fixture palette. The fast gate and the whole app suite (751 tests) passed.

- **Host and capture:** as in [the interface font check](#october-27-the-linux-interface-draws-its-real-weights); Settings at 1480 × 980 with Midnight, Daylight (Braden) and the Omarchy theme (Tokyo Night). For each: at rest, the pointer on the unchecked Show the project list, then on the checked Restore repository tabs on startup, then Tab onto the unchecked switch and Shift+Tab back to the checked one.
- **Builds:** base `8f2e8da`, the change's parent's product code (sha256 `a97c3c86…`), not `origin/main`, whose interface font and fixes differ on every screen; candidate `4401009` (`57d9d6185b1d50a7c0a3ff2e0c0ed1b9064849d1a84ca027fc0f9fa1102c729e`).
- **Review:** a read-only design reviewer measured all 30 frames. The ring is whole on all four sides, 1 px of card surface then 2 px of full accent, ending 3 px outside the track: 10.1:1 against the card in Midnight (#76deba on #1a1f2d), 5.6:1 in Daylight (#0b745e on #fefcfd), 7.1:1 in Tokyo Night (#7ba1f6 on #16151e), clear of the label. The hovered unchecked track moves #2e374a → #39455b in Midnight and #d3dae7 → #bbc7db in Daylight, exactly `hover` less `panel`, and #383b55 → #4e5376 in Tokyo Night; a hovered checked track and the resting frames are pixel-identical to the base, and nothing else differs.
- **Open:** a frame with the pointer on a switch's label, which the root's group should also lift; macOS and fractional scale factors.
- **Evidence:** [`docs/evidence/switch-focus-and-hover/`](../evidence/switch-focus-and-hover), base and candidate strips per theme, the five states left to right. Viewed at full resolution: only the fixture's Settings rows.

The candidate reads "2 changed files · file list 3.1 ms", then "3 working changes · 1 staged", "3 working changes · 1 staged · content read 4.9 ms", and "2 changed files" back in History, untimed because nothing was measured.

## October 27 the Linux interface draws its real weights

The open finding of approved item 4 in [full Omarchy support](../development/omarchy-full-support.md): every interface weight drew Regular on Linux. Owner decision, 2026-10-27: fix it in the way best for Linux and the project, legally. `ad04939` embeds six unmodified IBM Plex Sans 3.005 faces ([provenance](../../assets/fonts/ibm-plex-sans/README.md)), the family the toolkit names `.SystemUIFont` on Linux; `25433f7` gives the History navigation's section labels the 11 px medium DESIGN.md asks for, which the Regular-only font had hidden.

- **Host:** Asahi Omarchy 4.0.4-mac.1, Hyprland 0.56.2, native Wayland, scale 2, Omarchy Tokyo Night. Each launch had an empty absolute `XDG_CONFIG_HOME` whose `fontconfig` linked to the owner's; notifications were silenced (`omarchy-shell notifications setDnd on`, then `off`) for every frame.
- **Fixture:** the demo repository of the [stock-theme check](#october-27-every-stock-omarchy-theme-after-the-contrast-fixes), unchanged: `README.md` staged and edited again, `src/ActivityFeed.tsx` edited, `notes/東京 plan.txt` untracked.
- **Builds:** `cargo build --release --locked -p gitturtle`, `rustc 1.99.0`: base `de4a8a2` (sha256 `976d28de…`), `ad04939` (`98d3db80f33580cfe3df7ec4cb2ba538e4e5ae1c6e299c5e57c9c6903bd8d15f`) and `25433f7` (`90b78a37da7588e21efb4c4d877f1b16563510df86056f156ceb461cb0194feb`); each embeds its source revision.
- **Test:** `desktop_text::tests::bundled_interface_font_draws_each_weight_in_its_own_face` registers only the bundled faces and resolves `.SystemUIFont` through the toolkit's `TextSystem::resolve_font`: weights 400, 500, 600 and 700 resolve to four faces, each with a wider `m` than the last, and the two italics to two more; without the faces the family does not resolve. The fast gate passed at `ad04939` and `25433f7`.
- **Capture:** floating at 1480 × 980, History (commit `0739bd6`, `compact.png` selected), Compare (`src/theme.css`), Changes, Settings, the command palette and the F10 menu; tiled beside a throwaway foot window, since tiling ignores the 1,000 px minimum, History, Compare and Changes at 845 and 640 px and Compare at 875 px; Settings at 18 pt interface text. Full scale 2 throughout.
- **Review:** five read-only design reviewers compared every pair at full resolution; each pair shows the same state. The weights now read as their own faces: the repository title, the Settings title and panel headings semibold; the commit title, the selected navigation label, count chips, Staged and Unstaged, setting titles, the palette's row titles and the Compare path medium; menus stay Regular as designed. No clipping, overlap or baseline shift: text bottoms are identical, and Plex's capitals stand about 1.5 px taller. Plex sets narrower than the Adwaita Sans it replaces, so commit subjects that truncated now fit, "Restore repository tabs on startup" takes one line at 18 pt, and theme names that truncate at 18 pt end on whole letters. `notes/東京 plan.txt` still draws its CJK from an installed font.
- **Breakpoints:** the header's compact buttons and History's rail switch at fixed scaled widths, unchanged. Two are measured from the font: History's labelled toolbar now needs about 401 px of column (409 before), and the Compare review row about 533 px of preview (561 before; reviewer estimates from the font files). At 875 px the candidate shows the labelled review row, fitted with about 17 and 30 px to spare, where the base collapsed it to arrows and Options; at 845 and 640 px both collapse.
- **Launch:** exec to mapped window, the two builds alternating, nine launches each with a warm cache: base median 107 ms (97–129), candidate 115 ms (103–135), about 8 ms to register the six faces.
- **Taste, for the owner:** Settings' Git identity field labels stay Regular beside the now-medium Projects titles; disabled palette rows are as heavy as enabled ones, so color alone tells them apart; Plex draws `→` and `—` small in the status bar; the amber 11 px Unstaged badge is a little thinner; at 640 px the Changes empty state leaves "history." alone on its second line; CJK from the fallback font is lighter than Plex Medium beside it.
- **Evidence:** [`docs/evidence/linux-interface-font/`](../evidence/linux-interface-font), base (`de4a8a2`) and candidate crops of History's header and navigation and its inspector (`25433f7`), Changes and Settings (`25433f7`), Compare at 875 px, History at 640 px and Settings at 18 pt (`ad04939`). Every frame was viewed at full resolution for names, paths, notifications and other windows: clean; the only path is the fixture's, and its authors are fictional.
- **Desktop restored:** Tokyo Night, the background link to `0-winding-road.jpg`, no `theme-backgrounds/`, JetBrainsMono Nerd Font for `fc-match monospace`, notifications off as found.

## October 27 every stock Omarchy theme after the contrast fixes

Approved item 4 of [full Omarchy support](../development/omarchy-full-support.md): Omarchy's 22 stock themes in History, Compare, Changes, Settings, the command palette and the F10 menu, before and after the theme fixes `1adbdf4` through `7553ddb`, and after `9eb9a36` and `de4a8a2`, which this check prompted.

- **Host:** Asahi Omarchy 4.0.4-mac.1, Hyprland 0.56.2, native Wayland, scale 2. The window floated at 1480 × 980 on an empty workspace; each launch had an empty absolute `XDG_CONFIG_HOME` whose `fontconfig` linked to the owner's. Desktop notifications were silenced (`omarchy-shell notifications setDnd on`, then `off` again) for every frame kept.
- **Fixture:** `scripts/create-demo-repo.py --output /tmp/gitturtle-evidence/demo`, then `README.md` staged and edited again, `src/ActivityFeed.tsx` edited and `notes/東京 plan.txt` untracked. No Git identity, so the composer asks for one.
- **Builds:** `cargo build --release --locked -p gitturtle`, `rustc 1.99.0`: base `2ecb52a` (executable sha256 `fa9fff73…`), `7553ddb` (`43d29489…`), `9eb9a36` (`be05cc36…`) and `de4a8a2` (`976d28dec5b2dfb3622c43946e7d883f255cdf182b7aac22e30c2e1ef29b60c2`); each embeds its source revision.
- **Capture:** a scripted sweep switched the desktop with `OMARCHY_THEME_SKIP_BACKGROUND=1 omarchy theme set` and captured the six surfaces with `grim`, at half size (1 px per logical pixel), and the Changes and menu frames also at full scale 2. Settings with the built-in Midnight and Braden cards was captured from each build; a disabled Switch appears only while the Omarchy card is selected, so the sweep's Settings frames carry it (Follow system).
- **Review of `7553ddb`:** six read-only design reviewers measured sampled pixels in all 132 frames, including every frame an image limit hid on the 26th (Matte Black, Kanagawa's Settings, palette and menu, Last Horizon's and Lupine's palette and menu, Lumon's Settings and menu). All eight fixes held in every theme. The checked Switch knob went from 1.01–2.57:1 to 4.70–15.9:1 (Kanagawa, whose accent equals its text, 1.01 → 13.0; built-in Midnight 1.37 → 10.24 and Braden 2.28 → 5.66); the selected navigation label from 3.34 (Solitude) to at least 5.10; History's badge on the selected row from 3.50 (Catppuccin Latte) to at least 5.28; and disabled menu and palette rows, which read like enabled ones (Latte 6.50 against 6.48), to 2.2–3.8 against 7–20 for enabled rows. Menus sit 1.07–1.34 above the page.
- **Found and fixed:** menu separators kept `border`, 1.16:1 to 1.25:1 on the raised menu in Matte Black, Hackerman and Lumon (`9eb9a36`), and the menu's two shadow layers were painted over its edge, darkening the fitted edge to 1.15:1–1.29:1 along the bottom in six dark themes (`de4a8a2`).
- **Final build, full-resolution frames:** in all 22 themes the menu edge is one color on all four sides, 1.31–2.60:1 against the menu (the bottom was 1.15–1.29 before `de4a8a2`) and 1.40–2.91:1 against the page, and separators read 1.31–2.57:1; Everforest and Hackerman hold 1.31, as `Palette::raised_edge` fits the edge to exactly 1.3. No frame shows a notification or another window. "1 staged" and "Unstaged" measured 4.65–9.14:1 in eight themes; the 4.18–4.45 read on half-size frames was downscaling.
- **Open:** `FontWeight::MEDIUM`, which count chips and the selected navigation label ask for, renders as Regular here. Every interface weight resolves to Adwaita Sans Regular: Linux's `.SystemUIFont` is IBM Plex Sans, which is not installed, and the toolkit resolves its fallback fonts at weight 400. Taste items left for the owner: light-theme disabled controls read about 2:1 (Latte's Push 2.06), Lumon's disabled Open command and the disabled "Set your commit identity" sit on a 15% primary tint, the command palette is a dialog on the page background rather than raised, and a light theme's menu matches its canvas where it overlaps it.
- **Evidence:** [`docs/evidence/omarchy-stock-themes/`](../evidence/omarchy-stock-themes), base (`2ecb52a`) and candidate (`de4a8a2`) crops of Catppuccin Latte's History, Changes, palette and menu, Hackerman's menu, Kanagawa's Settings, and the built-in Midnight and Braden Settings. Each crop was viewed at full resolution for names, paths, hostnames and addresses: clean; the only path is the fixture's and the only author is the fixture's. Latte's Changes pair shows the Unstaged fix; its gutter numbers and count chip already passed, so they are unchanged there. No template privacy scan ran: this host has no template set, and the owner approved visual checks for demo-fixture frames.
- **Desktop restored:** Tokyo Night, the background link to `0-winding-road.jpg`, no `theme-backgrounds/`, JetBrainsMono Nerd Font for `fc-match monospace`, notifications on.

Not checked: hovered menu and History rows, dropdowns and tooltips on the raised surface, and current upstream x86-64 Omarchy.

## October 26 Omarchy editor, default terminal and a launch-or-focus binding

With no editor in Settings, Open repository in editor now opens the Omarchy editor, a new Open repository in terminal opens the desktop's default terminal, and Linux's Menu offers both beside Reveal. The README's launch-or-focus binding was checked too.

- **Host:** as in the entries below (Asahi Omarchy 4.0.4-mac.1, Hyprland 0.56.2, native Wayland). No `~/.local/state/omarchy/defaults/editor`, so Omarchy's editor is Neovim; `xdg-terminal-exec --print-id` answers `foot.desktop` from Omarchy's `hyprland-xdg-terminals.list`.
- **Builds:** `cargo build --release --locked -p gitturtle` of `bb98a57`'s sources without the editor's working directory (executable sha256 `b0c66333…`) and with it (`dd7915a20f3f292a6d1d7b40e713564fe1c00465a8b98ddb141823e5c18afac9`), `rustc 1.99.0`, each launched with an empty absolute `XDG_CONFIG_HOME` on the demo fixture.
- **Menu:** F10 showed Open repository in editor, Open repository in terminal and Reveal repository in file manager as a group after Compare revisions.
- **Editor:** Open repository in editor opened a foot window of class `org.omarchy.nvim` running `nvim <fixture>` in its own `app-graphical.slice` scope, and the banner read "Sent this repository to your Omarchy editor." On the second build, from an instance whose `PATH` had no Omarchy directory, it found `/usr/share/omarchy/bin/omarchy-launch-editor` and opened Neovim the same way, with its working directory in the fixture (the first build left Neovim in GitTurtle's own working directory, the reason for the change).
- **Terminal:** Open repository in terminal opened foot, titled with the fixture's path, whose `bash` had the fixture as its working directory.
- **Settings:** the External editor field showed the placeholder `code` and the description "Executable name or path, such as code. Menu → Open repository in editor opens the current folder; left empty on Omarchy, your Omarchy editor opens it."
- **Launch or focus:** with the QA window open and Chrome focused, `omarchy-launch-or-focus 'com.gitturtle.GitTurtle' 'uwsm-app -- gitturtle'`, what the README's binding runs, focused GitTurtle and started nothing. With no GitTurtle window, the same pattern with the QA build as its launch command (`uwsm-app -- env XDG_CONFIG_HOME=… PATH=… <build> <fixture>`) opened it. `gitturtle` itself is on the session's `PATH` (`~/.local/bin`, per `systemctl --user show-environment`). Super+Shift+T is unbound in `omarchy menu keybindings --print`.

Not checked: macOS's Terminal handoff, a desktop without `xdg-terminal-exec`, a configured editor's working directory, and the binding typed as a physical key.

## October 26 omarchy font set reaches code text at once

With **Use the desktop's monospace font** on, `omarchy font set` used to reach code text only when the GitTurtle window was focused again. Now it applies at once.

- **Host:** as in the entries below (Asahi Omarchy 4.0.4-mac.1, Hyprland 0.56.2, native Wayland, scale 2). Before the check the owner had no `~/.config/fontconfig/`; JetBrainsMono Nerd Font came from Omarchy's packaged default.
- **Before, on `5647245`'s release build** (executable sha256 `3e70dff0…`): on a first launch (empty absolute `XDG_CONFIG_HOME`, whose `fontconfig` linked to `~/.config/fontconfig`, so `fc-match` read the owner's configuration) the Compare diff of `src/theme.css` drew JetBrains Mono. With Chrome focused, `omarchy font set "iA Writer Mono S"` left it in JetBrains Mono; focusing GitTurtle again switched it to iA Writer Mono S.
- **After, on `2ecb52a`'s release build** (`cargo build --release --locked -p gitturtle`, executable sha256 `fa9fff7305e332e98b2ca3aaa048ab3c216494f0eb295c172bed0759c79a2869`, `rustc 1.99.0`), with an empty `~/.config/fontconfig/` created first so the QA link resolved: with Chrome focused throughout, `omarchy font set "iA Writer Mono S"` redrew the diff in iA Writer Mono S within 1.5 s, and `omarchy font set "JetBrainsMono Nerd Font"` redrew it in JetBrains Mono within 1.5 s. Sampling the app's child processes every 100 ms for 5 s after each switch found no `fc-match`, so the refresh's own read did not trigger another.
- **Restored:** the terminal configurations `omarchy font set` rewrites (Alacritty, kitty, foot) were copied back from backups taken before the check, and the created `~/.config/fontconfig/` was removed; `fc-match monospace` answers JetBrainsMono Nerd Font again.

`desktop_text::user_fontconfig::tests` cover a configuration home without `fontconfig/` (creating it with `fonts.conf`, as `omarchy font set` does, is a change, and so is a later rewrite) and that reads are not changes. Not checked natively: a desktop with no `fontconfig/` at launch (the QA link needs its target to exist), and a `font-set` hook.

## October 26 list Copy in Working Changes and file history, and Select All everywhere

Copy Selected List Item now works in Working Changes and File History's revision list, and Ctrl+A, which Omarchy 4.5's Super+A sends, was checked on every surface.

- **Host and build:** as in the entry below (Asahi Omarchy 4.0.4-mac.1, Hyprland 0.56.2, native Wayland, scale 2, Tokyo Night, fcitx5 running). `cargo build --release --locked -p gitturtle` of `5647245`'s sources (executable sha256 `3e70dff04e0d10ddc86c708943904e45904d9a6cf736f7d31de698823eb96500`, `rustc 1.99.0`), launched with an empty absolute `XDG_CONFIG_HOME` on a `scripts/create-demo-repo.py` fixture with `README.md` staged and edited again, `src/ActivityFeed.tsx` edited and `notes/東京 plan.txt` untracked, in a floating 1480 × 980 window. Keys through `wtype` unless noted; clicks through the scratch virtual pointer.
- **Working Changes:** Ctrl+2 then Ctrl+A selected all four rows, and Ctrl+C copied exactly `README.md\nsrc/ActivityFeed.tsx\nnotes/東京 plan.txt`: list order, `README.md` once although it was selected in both areas. Clicking `ActivityFeed.tsx` and pressing Ctrl+C copied `src/ActivityFeed.tsx`.
- **File History:** from `src/theme.css` in `0739bd6`, File history, a click on the second revision and Ctrl+C copied `e96f692bccb6bfe907efb8ae6a9b30434dedea75`, which `git rev-parse e96f692` matches.
- **Select All (Ctrl+A):** in the focused commit message it highlighted the whole message, and Ctrl+C copied exactly `git log -1 --format=%B e96f692`. In the Compare diff it selected the whole patch (Ctrl+C copied its 19 lines from `--- a/src/theme.css`). In the commit composer's Title it selected the typed draft, which the next typing replaced. In Working Changes it selected every visible row (above). In the History list and the revision list, which hold one selection, and on Settings opened with its gear button and no field focused, it changed nothing, and Ctrl+C in those lists still copied the full hash. (A first Settings check was void: on this host `wtype`'s Ctrl+comma reached the app as Ctrl+O and opened the folder picker, so it tested the portal's dialog instead; the recheck on `2ecb52a`'s build, in Catppuccin Latte, changed no pixel but the pointer's.) Text fields elsewhere use the same toolkit binding as the Title field; History search was checked in the entry below.
- **Omarchy's dispatched keys:** after `hyprctl switchxkblayout apple-mtp-keyboard 0` restored the physical keyboard's keymap, which `wtype` had replaced, `hl.dsp.send_key_state` Ctrl+2, Ctrl+A and Ctrl+C, as Omarchy's clipboard bindings send them, opened Working Changes, selected all and copied the same three paths.

Not checked: a physical Super+A (Omarchy 4.0.4 has no such binding; 4.5 adds it), macOS's Command-A and Command-C on these lists, and Ctrl+A in the Blame and navigation lists, which take the same one-selection list binding as the History list.

## October 26 selectable commit text, the new application ID and Omarchy polish

The commit inspector's message and metadata became selectable and copyable, list Copy started to work in History and the changed-file list, the application ID became `com.gitturtle.GitTurtle`, Settings gained Page Up, Page Down, Home and End, and History's empty search message stays in view in narrow windows.

- **Host:** as in the entry below (Asahi Omarchy 4.0.4-mac.1, Hyprland 0.56.2, native Wayland, scale 2, Tokyo Night, fcitx5 running). Each launch had an empty absolute `XDG_CONFIG_HOME` and the `scripts/create-demo-repo.py` fixture, in a floating 1480 × 980 window unless noted. Pointer input went through a scratch `zwlr_virtual_pointer_v1` client, keys through `wtype`, and Omarchy's universal clipboard through `hl.dsp.send_key_state` exactly as `default/hypr/bindings/clipboard.lua` sends it.
- **Builds:** `./scripts/package-linux.sh` on `1b12f0f` (executable sha256 `e4b1249e…`, archive `54700387…`) for the application ID, opacity, Settings and empty-state checks, and on `6d0180b` (executable sha256 `44641f5b…`, archive `e92281d1…`) for the selection checks. Both report `source_tree` `modified` only because an agent worktree directory was untracked under `.claude/worktrees/`; `git diff HEAD` was empty. Release, `aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu`, `rustc 1.99.0`.
- **Application ID:** `install.py` over an installed `com.gitturtle.desktop` build removed its desktop file and icons and installed `com.gitturtle.GitTurtle.desktop` and its icons. `gtk-launch com.gitturtle.GitTurtle` opened the app; Hyprland reported class and initial class `com.gitturtle.GitTurtle` with Omarchy's `default-opacity` tag and opacity 0.985. The [Linux guide's](../linux.md#arch-linux-and-omarchy) rule in `~/.config/hypr/hyprland.lua` removed the tag and gave opacity 1; with the file restored byte for byte, 0.985 returned.
- **Settings keys:** from the focus Settings opens with, Home, Page Down and End moved the page to its top, by most of a screen and to its bottom.
- **Narrow History:** at the 1000 px window minimum the 540 px list scrolls its columns sideways; a search with no match centered "No matching commits" in the visible list.
- **Live theme:** with the Omarchy theme selected, `omarchy theme set` to Catppuccin Latte, Gruvbox and back to Tokyo Night retinted the open window each time within about a second.
- **Selection:** an I-beam showed over the message; a drag from the body's first word into its third line highlighted the wrapped lines, and Ctrl+C copied exactly `Keep this experiment … branch switching.\nThe original das` (the soft wrap as a space, the line break kept). Omarchy's dispatched Ctrl+C copied the same. Ctrl+A then Ctrl+C copied exactly `git log -1 --format=%B`'s message. A drag across the author into the date copied `era\nSep 1, 2026 at 10:28`. Clicking another commit cleared the highlight. The highlight kept the text readable on Catppuccin Latte, White and Tokyo Night, and the selection survived those live theme switches.
- **List copy:** with the History list focused on `52f471a` and `9ee82b2`, Ctrl+C copied each full 40-character hash; with the changed-file list focused, it copied `src/ActivityFeed.tsx`.

Not checked: macOS (Cmd+C, Cmd+A and the Edit menu), a physical Super+C in the commit message (synthetic dispatch only), auto-scroll during a drag, and release-mode timing. Mixing `wtype` with `hl.dsp.send_key_state` in one test made dispatched keys act as other keys: after `wtype` had uploaded its own keymap (in which keycode 1 is the key it last typed), a dispatched Ctrl+A reached the app as keycode 1 and acted as Ctrl+C. Use one input tool per sequence.

## October 26 a first launch on Omarchy matches the desktop

A launch with no preference store on an Omarchy desktop now starts with the Omarchy theme and **Use the desktop's monospace font** on, and saves made before a store exists keep those settings (the [persistence contract](../../crates/app/docs/writes-and-persistence.md#preferences-and-commit-drafts) has the rule). Before this change the first window was Midnight, and opening the first repository saved `"theme": "midnight"`.

- **Host:** 2026-10-26, the owner's MacBook Pro (M2 Pro) running the Asahi port of Omarchy (`omarchy version` 4.0.4-mac.1, AArch64), Hyprland 0.56.2 on native Wayland, a 3456 × 2160 panel at scale 2. Omarchy theme Tokyo Night, fontconfig `monospace` JetBrainsMono Nerd Font, fcitx5 running.
- **Build:** `./scripts/package-linux.sh dist/omarchy-defaults-1` on `a755d60` with this change's Rust sources uncommitted (`--build-info`: `source_tree` `modified`, `aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu`, release, `rustc 1.99.0`). Executable sha256 `e65ede0b982bd9dff9e512ad8a60cbe8273bafeb2614994b9ef1d1389fe8aee3`, archive sha256 `8dfd41da0d2ea65a97042c581f784dd4ba7ec68e7353c53bb279a034cee0b46a`. Only Markdown changed after the build. Installed over the previous build with `install.py`.
- **First launch:** the owner's `~/.config/gitturtle` was moved aside, so the store was missing, and GitTurtle was opened from its launcher entry. The first window (Projects) drew Tokyo Night's background and accent. Settings (Ctrl+,) showed the **Omarchy** card selected, captioned "Follows Tokyo Night", and **Follow system appearance** off and disabled, described "Omarchy follows your desktop theme". Quitting saved no `preferences.json`.
- **First write:** relaunched on a `scripts/create-demo-repo.py` fixture, still with no store. Remembering the repository wrote the first `preferences.json`, with `"theme": "omarchy"` and `"system_code_font": true`. A third launch read it back unchanged. History and Compare drew Tokyo Night, and diff code was drawn in JetBrains Mono.
- **Restored:** the owner's store was put back with its recents and session. Its theme was then set to Omarchy with the desktop font on, because an existing store keeps its stored theme by design.
- **Re-check on the committed fix:** `./scripts/package-linux.sh dist/omarchy-defaults-2` on `6f33451` (`source_tree` `clean`), executable sha256 `4c1e3056e7e09997b854e1aef5cb7c2df6a51e1626da4ef2c61542d4b2926b7f`, archive sha256 `c3909333ad7cd43186116d684e9d91ce125e83bb814935e35488e94df8c06093`, installed with `install.py`. Launched on the demo fixture with an empty absolute `XDG_CONFIG_HOME` and the real HOME: History drew Tokyo Night, and the first `preferences.json` held `"theme": "omarchy"` and `"system_code_font": true`. The owner's store was not touched.

The universal-clipboard checks from the same session are recorded in the [Omarchy plan](../development/omarchy-full-support.md#phases). The physical Super+C/V/X check is still open there. Not checked: x86-64 Omarchy, current upstream Omarchy (v4.0.6 or 4.5), and a first launch with an unreadable or non-regular `colors.toml`, which unit tests cover.

## October 25 Linux AArch64 on Omarchy on Asahi Linux

The Linux packager and installer refused every architecture but x86-64. Commit `a284a00` makes the packager target the machine it runs on (`aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu` here, bundle `dist/gitturtle-linux-aarch64`), lets package identity check the AArch64 ELF machine, and has the installer accept AArch64 and refuse a bundle built for the other architecture. No product code changed, and no CI job builds for AArch64 Linux.

- **Host:** 2026-10-25, the owner's Apple MacBook Pro (16-inch, M2 Pro, 2023) running Omarchy 4.0.4 on Asahi Linux (Arch Linux ARM, kernel 7.1.12, AArch64); Hyprland 0.56.2 (`efb5099`) on native Wayland; Mesa 26.2.2 with the Asahi Vulkan driver (`vulkan-asahi`, Apple M2 Pro G14S); a 3456 × 2160 panel at scale 2. Omarchy on Asahi already had every native library the build checks for and `clang`. Rust 1.99.0 came from rustup through mise (the checkout's `rust-toolchain.toml`), cmake 4.4.4 from mise, and Pillow 13.0.0 was installed into mise's Python 3.15.1, instead of the pacman packages the guide names.
- **Builds:** first, a release of `2d0e3e0` (clean, before the packaging change) with `cargo build --release --locked -p gitturtle --target aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu --target-dir target`: 630 crates, no warnings, every library resolved by `ldd`. The packager's rebuild replaced that executable before its sha256 was recorded. Then `./scripts/package-linux.sh` at `a284a00` built and packaged a release whose `--build-info` reads `a284a00`, `source_tree` `clean`, `aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu`, profile `release`, `rustc 1.99.0 (b940084d7 2026-09-28)`; executable sha256 `5d4ca9d50d8b8a24b4176ff68d594f9031c91029c6def58ac690ffc4863b8c59`, archive sha256 `6365d3e1bc59b4ee7453c89b5404ce39e7a31977004fb07dbc14eedfcaad2523`. It is a development bundle: license review lists `mac-0.1.1` and `ufbx-0.11.3`, the same gaps the x86-64 Linux bundle records. Its packaging tree reads `modified` because this record and the guide's AArch64 section were uncommitted drafts at the time, so the bundle's `README.md` is that draft of `docs/linux.md`.
- **Checks:** `python3 scripts/test-install-linux.py --bundle dist/gitturtle-linux-aarch64` passed all 22 tests, including installation, upgrade and rollback in disposable homes. `python3 -B -m unittest discover -s scripts/ci/tests -p 'test_*.py'` ran 361 tests with 7 skipped, against 358 with 15 skipped on `2d0e3e0`: the packager refusal tests, which skipped on AArch64 before, now run. `python3 scripts/gate.py fast` passed format and the workspace check; `typos` found nothing.
- **Installation:** `python3 dist/gitturtle-linux-aarch64/install.py`, without sudo, into an account with no earlier GitTurtle installation. The installed `~/.local/bin/gitturtle` has the executable's sha256 and the same `--build-info`, and the installer validated the launcher entry `com.gitturtle.desktop.desktop`.
- **Native:** each launch ran on a temporary 1480 × 800 headless output at scale 1, placed there by a runtime window rule so the owner's workspace kept its focus, with a fresh HOME and XDG directories, the `GitTurtle QA <qa@example.invalid>` identity and `TZ=UTC`, opening a `scripts/create-demo-repo.py` fixture. The owner's GitTurtle settings and Omarchy state were not read. The window was a native Wayland client (not XWayland) each time, and stderr stayed empty. With the `2d0e3e0` build, History drew the commit graph, branch groups, inspector and changed files; Return, after checking that the active window was the app's, opened `public/panels/compact.png` in Compare, side by side, with the 720 × 440 PNG on the After side and an empty Before side. Ctrl+Shift+P sent through `wtype` did not open the command palette in that run, although Return reached the app, so the palette stays unchecked rather than failed. The installed `a284a00` executable drew the same History. After each run, `hyprctl monitors -j` matched its reading from before. Frames stay local, because this host has no privacy templates.

Not checked on AArch64: the folder picker, Git writes and network actions, fractional scaling, the Omarchy theme, the command palette, and any other ARM hardware or distribution.

## October 6 the branch chooser, the remote manager, Tags and Push to… cue overflow with the always-visible scrollbar

Task `tab-lists-scroll-cues`, attempt 2, candidate `0cc2fb0` on base `5412c77` (the run's accepted head, designated by the coordinator as the contract's "origin/main when the task starts"; the candidate is the controller's rebase of attempt 2 onto `5412c77`, committed at 23:19 UTC on October 5, and was not rebased again for this round). On the base the branch chooser (`branch-chooser-list`), the remote manager (`remote-manager-list`), Tags (`tags-list`) and the tag inspector's Push to… list (`tag-remote-list`) scroll through their FocusReveal handle but draw no scrollbar, so nothing shows that rows lie beyond the edge. The candidate gives all four the always-visible vertical scrollbar of the review dialogs through a shared `tags::ScrollCue` (`crates/app/src/tags.rs`), with the ids `branch-chooser-scrollbar`, `remote-manager-scrollbar`, `tags-scrollbar` and `tag-remote-scrollbar`. While a list overflows, its rows keep a gutter of `Scrollbar::width()` (16 px) plus the installed ring's gap and width (1 + 2 px) at their right, and the track runs in it over the rows' column, inside the room the list keeps around its rows; a list whose rows fit keeps no gutter and draws no scrollbar. The remote manager keeps no room around its cards (each card keeps its controls' ring room inside its own padding), so its column is the list itself.

Automated, in `cargo test --locked -p gitturtle` (the controller's gates on this rebased candidate passed at 23:22 UTC on October 5: guidance, format, check, `cargo test --locked --workspace` with 1,131 passed, 0 failed and 5 ignored, and Clippy): the candidate adds `branch_chooser_cues_rows_beyond_its_edge`, `branch_chooser_that_fits_paints_no_thumb`, `remote_manager_cues_remotes_beyond_its_edge` and `remote_manager_that_fits_paints_no_thumb` (`crates/app/src/branch_actions/tests.rs`), and `tags_list_cues_rows_beyond_its_edge`, `tags_list_that_fits_paints_no_thumb`, `push_list_cues_buttons_beyond_its_edge` and `push_list_that_fits_paints_no_thumb` (`crates/app/src/tags.rs`), on the shared `assert_scroll_cue` and `assert_no_scroll_cue`: at 13 and 18 pt in a 1000 × 680 window, a thumb whose position follows the offset at the top, half and end of the reach, a track that overlaps no row's ring footprint, and, for a list whose rows fit, no thumb and rows at the list's full width. `push_list_that_fits_paints_no_thumb` is the supporting evidence for the one state the native fixture cannot show (below).

`DESIGN.md` gains a paragraph under the navigation rules: "A scrolling list of tab-stop rows shows the always-visible scrollbar that cues overflow in the review dialogs (Reflog's content and changed files, the discard and forced worktree removal reviews) and in the commit inspector's message. The branch chooser, the remote manager, Tags and the tag inspector's Push to… list each keep, while their rows overflow, a gutter of the 16 px track plus the focus ring's 3 px of room at the right of their rows."

Native evidence, full tier: base and candidate in one session. Let `$EVIDENCE` be `/tmp/gitturtle-evidence`.
- **Builds:** base `5412c77` (`5412c77d3362bf8a696007b1e82bf286335539b4`, sha256 `88033df6a30eb00e1e5e31aa3b7d15790fa027b5835b107a3c951be66b605016`) and candidate `0cc2fb0` (`0cc2fb05da45e73aeab7e6fc18bc24f223875be1`, sha256 `8291fde3ced9c60b9748417f8ef1cd2bdc230d20e8fd495815654e0820e89c91`). `--build-info` of each: GitTurtle 0.1.0, `source_tree` `clean`, `x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu`, profile `release`, `rustc 1.99.0 (b940084d7 2026-09-28)`, `build_unix_seconds` 1791245313 (base, 00:08:33 UTC) and 1791248968 (candidate, 01:09:28 UTC). Both are clean release builds made by the coordinator with `scripts/operator/build-release.sh`, each in its own fresh `CARGO_TARGET_DIR` deleted once its executable was copied (`.target-gitturtle-base-5412c77.f1Wvxu`, 00:07 to 00:10 UTC, and `.target-gitturtle-cand-0cc2fb0.FtJ21T`, 01:08 to 01:11 UTC), with `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=8` because this host's compilers crashed at the default parallelism that night; `qa.py identity` accepted the pair at 01:11:51 UTC.
- **Host:** 2026-10-06, Ubuntu 26.04.1 LTS, GNOME 50.1, Linux X11 through XWayland `:0` at scale factor 1, window 1000 × 680, `--input mutter`: every key, click, press, motion and wheel step went through Mutter RemoteDesktop with X focus verified, never XTest. `qa.py display-check` found the desktop unlocked and no GitTurtle process or QA driver before the first launch (01:11:56 UTC) and after the last (02:14:28 UTC). Side work during the window: the unattended loop ran another task's implementer session from 01:12 UTC, its gates from 01:35 and its reviews until 01:40 UTC, then idled, and the coordinator release-built that task's candidate from 01:40 to 01:46 UTC on CPUs 0-7 and 10-17, which overlaps the first three minutes of the evidence run; the tooling and the app it launched were pinned to CPUs 8, 9, 18 and 19 (`taskset`) throughout. No timing claim rests on this run. The QA tooling ran from the checkout at `8d3fccd`.
- **Fixture:** the spec's recipe at `$EVIDENCE/fixtures/tab-lists-scroll-cues/repo` (recipe sha256 `643134ee1cb2…`): the tab-reveals fixture's operations unchanged, five commits (HEAD `75842df` on `main`), 30 local branches (`main`, `topic-01` to `topic-29`), 30 tags (`v1.00` to `v1.29`, the even ones annotated) and a local bare `origin` holding `main`, plus twelve empty local bare remotes, `mirror-01` to `mirror-12`, so the remote manager lists 13 remotes and the inspector 13 Push to… buttons (`git remote` order: the mirrors, then `origin`). Its expected object IDs held, and every launch left HEAD, status, index, refs, reflogs and each bare remote's refs, reflogs and configuration unchanged. Nothing fetched, pulled or pushed, and no Push to… button was pressed in any build.
- **Route, the same input in both builds:**
  - **Branch chooser:** a click on the toolbar's current-branch button ((55, 112) at 13 pt, (60, 156) at 18 pt). Up selects the menu's last item, Manage remotes…; Up ×11 reaches Manage another branch…, guarded by exactly two changed highlight rows (y 309–326 and 545–562 at 13 pt, 274–298 and 587–611 at 18 pt); then Return.
  - **Remote manager:** the same menu. Down ×11 brings the highlight back, guarded as identical to the frame after the first Up left of the menu's own scrollbar (x < 185); then Return.
  - **Tags:** the palette's "browse and manage tags".
  - **Tag inspector:** Tags filtered to `v1.01`, its one row focused and guarded, then a click on that row (Return there reaches the dialog's default action, Done; see the findings). Two guards: Tags is gone, and a dialog is open.
  - **In each list:** `top` as opened.
  - **Fits:** Tab to the filter, type the filter query, guarded by the list having changed; Tab to the first control for `fits`; Shift+Tab and BackSpace restore every row.
  - **Focus and hover:** Tab to the first control for `focus`, guarded by its ring; a glide onto it for `focus-hover`; a glide onto the thumb at the top for `thumb-hover`; then a park in mouse mode, so the scrollbar sees the pointer leave.
  - **Middle and end:** Tab to the control whose reveal puts the list nearest half its reach for `middle`, then to the last control for `end`, each guarded by its ring at the lower edge.
  - **Drag:** a glide onto the thumb at the end, the button down, two motions to 100 px above, `thumb-drag` with the button still held, the release, a park, and Escape.

  The remote manager reveals its last Remove… with the ring's room only, so its `end` adds three wheel steps over the list; the dialog body did not move. The Push to… list is hovered, never pressed or dragged. 31 guards per launch; all 248 passed.
- **Variants:** Midnight and Porcelain at 13 and 18 pt; 8 launches, 184 crops.
- **Preparation runs (candidate only, no frame committed, not evidence):**
  1. `$EVIDENCE/runs/rehearsal-tab-lists-scroll-cues-1` (01:12:03 UTC): refused before any launch, the binary path was relative.
  2. `…-2` (01:12:18–01:13:40 UTC): stopped inconclusive at the remote manager's Down ×11 guard. 6,874 px differed in a strip at x 189–205, the menu's own scrollbar fading out, with the highlight back on Manage remotes…; Escape was sent and nothing was chosen. The guard's region was narrowed to x < 185.
  3. `…-3` (01:14:27–01:28:48 UTC): passed, 4 launches, 464 of 464 recorded. It measured the 18 pt geometry and menu rows, and found that `mirror-1` (three cards) still overflows at 18 pt and that Return in the filtered Tags dialog closes it through Done instead of inspecting the row. After that Return the inspector section ran on the History page, where only Tab, pointer motion and Escape were sent.
  4. `…-4` (01:33:20–01:34:32 UTC, Midnight 13 and 18 pt): opened the inspector by a click and recorded 16 Tabs: Copy object ID, Delete local tag…, then the 13 Push to… buttons and Done.
  5. `…-5` (01:36:46–01:42:49 UTC, Midnight 13 and 18 pt): the final route with the candidate's thresholds, 192 of 192 as expected.
- **Evidence run:** `qa.py scenario run` of `scenario.json` (sha256 `9633f1f0fb8f539269293d2fb7761ea14c9d5530fef78d45cc364eae6b1b0ebf`, the committed spec byte for byte) with both builds into `$EVIDENCE/runs/tab-lists-scroll-cues`, 2026-10-06 01:43:09–02:09:23 UTC: exit 0, verdict pass. 8 launches ok with no warnings, 828 of 828 evaluations as expected (504 recorded only), and none of the 184 crops matched a privacy template.
- **Coordinator decisions (01:08 UTC), applied:**
  1. Identity first, then the rehearsal, then the regenerated spec and one evidence run.
  2. "A list whose rows fit" is shown on the chooser, the remote manager and Tags, with `push_list_that_fits_paints_no_thumb` as supporting test evidence.
  3. Push to… is hovered only, never pressed or dragged.
  4. Clearance is measured from the ring to the thumb and from the ring to the track's left edge, derived below.
  5. The thresholds were fixed before the run: 1.3 at rest and 3.0 hovered or pressed.
  6. All 184 crops are kept.
  7. The middle is reached by Tab, with a focused row.
  8. No mask or retry inside a run; every run is named.
  9. The spec is kept byte for byte.

Frames in [`evidence/tab-lists-scroll-cues/`](../evidence/tab-lists-scroll-cues), the scenario's 184 crops (5,678,560 bytes; no two byte-identical), each a dialog-region crop of the 1000 × 680 window, never the whole window. Boxes are window pixels; sha256 prefixes in parentheses; `commit-manifest.json` in the bundle holds each crop's full sha256, box and source frame. The `fits` crops reach below their shortened dialogs onto the History page (the fixture's commits); the Tags and Remotes crops cut their dialog titles at the top edge.
